### [32] [AnchorKV: Lossless Key-Value Cache Reconstruction from Layer-Sparse Residual Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00117)
*Hao Li,Jiashu Zhang,Kaleem Ullah Qasim,Heying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: AnchorKV 提出"存储—重放谱"（storage-replay spectrum）：通过可配置的残差锚点，在"逐层残差缓存"与"完全重算"之间连续插值，从而无损重建 Transformer 的 KV cache。其核心是架构相关的盈亏平衡规则（仅凭模型 config 即可算出首个省内存的锚点间距）与级联重放算法（把重建复杂度从 O(L·s) 降到 O(L)）；对交错滑窗架构用精确动态规划按非均匀重建需求布置锚点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文的多轮会话需要"可复活"(reactivatable) 的 KV cache：传统做法要么保存全部 KV（内存开销随上下文线性膨胀，限制并发会话数），要么完全重算（prefill 代价高）。已有工作表明残差流缓存可无损重建 KV，但其内存收益强依赖模型架构（MHA/GQA/MLA/滑窗等），缺乏可解释、可移植的设计规则与高效的部署级实现。

Method: 1) 抽象出以"锚点间距 s"为参数的存储—重放谱：s=1 退化为逐层残差缓存，s=∞ 退化为全重算，部署时按显存预算选工作点；2) 推导架构相关的盈亏平衡间距，可直接由模型配置文件计算，无需测量；3) 级联重放：利用相邻锚点间的增量关系链式重建，将重建代价由 O(L·s) 降为 O(L)（L 为层数）；4) 对交错滑窗架构，把锚点放置建模为精确动态规划，在非均匀重建需求下最优布点；5) 在 CPU、CUDA、昇腾 NPU 三后端，6 类架构族，fp32/bf16 下验证逐比特一致的重建结果与不变的后续 logits/token。

Result: 4k 上下文下，级联重建比朴素重放快至多 16.3×；32k 上下文下，在 8 个适用的全局注意力 checkpoint 上，最深间距带来 5.78–54.0× 的全 KV 可复活会话容量（等价于并发/片上会话数的内存增益）。与 7 种外部 cache 方法在同一模型上对比，AnchorKV 保持全缓存检索与 QA 分数；有损方法则改变输出保真度与任务分数。盈亏平衡间距仅凭 config 文件即可算出。

Conclusion: AnchorKV 把"残差缓存 vs 重算"从二选一变成可调参数化的设计空间，并给出可预测、可静态计算的部署规则与 O(L) 的工程化重建路径；在异构加速器上与训练/精度无关地保持 bit-exact，因而可直接嵌入现有推理栈作为会话级 KV 复用/换出机制。其价值在于以极小存储换取长上下文高并发会话容量，属"以计算换内存（compute-for-memory）"的系统层优化，而非近似压缩。

Abstract: Residual-stream caching can reconstruct transformer key-value (KV) caches losslessly, but its memory advantage depends on architecture. We present AnchorKV, a storage-replay spectrum connecting per-layer residual caching and full recomputation through configurable residual anchors. An architecture-dependent break-even rule identifies the first storage-saving spacing, while deployment selects an operating point from the spectrum and cascade replay reduces reconstruction from O(Ls) to O(L), with L the model depth and s the anchor spacing. For interleaved sliding-window architectures, exact dynamic programming places anchors under non-uniform reconstruction demand. Across CPU, CUDA, and Ascend NPU backends, six architecture families, and fp32/bf16 arithmetic, evaluations verify bit-exact KV reconstruction and unchanged continuation logits and tokens. At 4k context, cascade reconstruction is up to 16.3 times faster than naive replay; across eight applicable global-attention checkpoints at 32k context, deepest spacing yields 5.78-54.0 times full-KV reactivatable session capacity. Compared with seven external cache methods on a common model, AnchorKV preserves full-cache retrieval and question-answering scores, whereas lossy methods change output fidelity and task scores. The break-even spacing is computable from a model's configuration file alone.

</details>


### [33] [CAPER: Circuit-Aware Parameter-Efficient Reasoning in LLMs for Analog Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00187)
*Saion K. Roy*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CAPER：一种"电路感知"的PEFT方法，在LoRA旁路之外增加一个小分支，读取LLM中每一级电路（stage）的属性并将其跨相关stage组合，从而使LLM能学会判断模拟电路在PVT变化下的隐性失效与反馈环路极性等结构性验证问题，而LoRA和高秩适配器在此类任务上仅接近随机猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被用于生成模拟电路网表，但每个网表都必须验证：需要发现不满足的规格以及在PVT（工艺、电压、温度）变化下才暴露的可靠性问题。这类问题的根源是"各级电路如何组合"的结构性关系，而LoRA等通用PEFT方法只学习局部参数增量，无法显式建模stage间的组合语义，因此在该任务上失效。

Method: 提出CAPER（circuit-aware PEFT）：在原有LoRA分支旁并联一个小的附加分支，该分支从LLM的隐藏表示中读取每一级（stage）的属性，并仅对相关的stage做组合/聚合，从而把电路拓扑级的知识注入微调过程。作者在两个代表性任务上评估：(1) 判定反馈环路是否为负反馈；(2) 对比较器锁存器（comparator latch）标记在PVT变化下才出现的潜在失效（latent failure）。基线与对照包括LoRA以及高秩（high-rank）适配器。

Result: 负反馈判定任务中，LoRA与高秩适配器停留在随机水平，CAPER在所有测试用例上全部正确。在比较器锁存器任务中，90个标称条件下可工作的LLM生成设计里有11个在PVT变化下失效，CAPER能学会标记这类潜在失效，而通用适配器仍接近随机。

Conclusion: 结果表明：把领域结构知识（stage级属性及其组合）显式引入PEFT，可使原本依赖通用适配器无法学习的模拟电路验证检查变得可通过微调学到。其含义是"结构归纳偏置"比单纯增加适配器秩更有效，为LLM作为模拟电路验证助手提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate analog circuits, yet every netlist must also be verified. We propose LLMs as verification assistants for analog circuits that flag unmet specifications and reliability issues that appear under process, voltage, and temperature (PVT) variation. These issues emerge from how circuit stages combine, a structure that generic parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as LoRA do not capture. To this end, we introduce CAPER, a circuit-aware PEFT method that adds a small branch alongside LoRA to read each stage's properties from the LLM and combine them from the relevant stages. In deciding whether a feedback loop is negative, LoRA and a high-rank adapter stay at chance, whereas CAPER answers every test case correctly. For comparator latches, where 11 of 90 LLM-written designs that work under nominal conditions fail under PVT variation, CAPER likewise learns to flag such latent failures, while generic adapters stay near chance. These results show that adding circuit knowledge to PEFT makes such analog verification checks learnable through fine-tuning.

</details>


### [34] [FieldRT: Reconfigurable Architecture from Geometric Sensitivity to Streaming Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00476)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FieldRT 提出一种算法-硬件协同设计方法，以统一的“几何敏感性+数据生命周期”分析同时推导部署压缩剖面（deployment profile）与其空间硬件实现，并在 AFN（亚百万参数定向 BEV 检测器）上验证：任务相关冗余而非均匀冗余，粗粒度指标稳定不代表严格 IoU 精度不退化，单独安全的变换组合后可能有害；最终在 AMD Kria KV260 的 PL 上以参数化流式架构共享空间算子的场访问与存储，实现生产级数值闭合，并使多膨胀率（multi-dilation）执行可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘侧几何感知需同时满足定位精度与严格延迟/内存/能耗/精度约束。传统流程是“先用聚合质量或复杂度代理指标压缩模型，再映射到固定加速器接口”的两段式解耦：粗粒度指标下看似冗余但对精确定位关键的几何结构会被误删，而保留下来的几何结构中的硬件级效率（数据复用、存储共享、流式访问）又未被利用。定向 BEV 检测中全局上下文、多尺度交互与几何融合同时影响预测质量与硬件数据流，使这种割裂的代价尤为明显。

Method: 核心是单一分析驱动两端：用几何敏感性（哪类几何结构对严格定位精度敏感）与数据生命周期（中间特征/场的生存期）作为共同依据，直接导出部署剖面（容量与组织方式）及其空间实现。以 Axis-Fusion Network（AFN，<1M 参数的定向 BEV 检测器）为载体构造受控剖面，改变其几何处理的容量与组织；再将保留下来的几何结构映射为参数化流式架构（parameterized streaming architecture），在空间算子间共享 field access 与 storage，形成定制 PL 原语（面向 AMD Kria KV260）。

Result: 受控实验表明：(1) 冗余是任务相关且指标间不均匀的——稠密预测指标可提升而真实实例检测退化，粗定位稳定但严格 IoU 精度下降；(2) 单独安全的变换组合后可能变得有害（非加性/交互效应）。硬件侧，定制 PL 原语在 KV260 上达到生产规模的数值闭合（numerical closure），并使原本不可行的 multi-dilation 执行变为可实现。

Conclusion: 该工作把“压缩决策”和“硬件映射”从串行解耦改为由几何敏感性与数据生命周期共同驱动的协同设计，指出以聚合代理指标压缩几何模型存在系统性风险，需要指标感知、组合感知的评估。工程上，参数化流式+算子间场共享的 PL 数据流为边缘定向 BEV 检测提供了可行的低延迟、低内存路径，并提示在多膨胀率卷积等结构化几何算子上按数据生命周期做存储规划可显著降低片上/片外流量。

Abstract: Geometric perception on edge must simultaneously achieve two goals that are rarely addressed together: preserve accurate spatial understanding for object localization and operate within stringent latency, memory, energy, and precision constraints. This coupling is particularly evident in oriented bird's-eye-view (BEV) detection, where global context, multi-scale interactions, and geometric fusion affect both prediction quality and hardware dataflow. Conventional deployment flows first compress a model using aggregate quality or complexity proxies and then map the compressed operations to a fixed accelerator interface. This separation risks removing geometric structures that appear redundant under coarse metrics but are critical for precise localization, while leaving hardware-level efficiencies in the retained geometry unexploited. We present FieldRT, an algorithm-hardware co-design methodology that uses a common analysis of geometric sensitivity and data lifetime to derive both the deployment profile and its spatial realization. We deploy FieldRT with Axis-Fusion Network (AFN), a sub-million-parameter oriented-BEV detector, and construct controlled profiles that vary the capacity and organization of its geometric processing. The experiments show that redundancy is task-dependent rather than uniform across metrics. Dense prediction can improve while real instance detection deteriorates; coarse localization may remain stable while strict-IoU precision degrades. Moreover, transformations that are individually safe can become detrimental when combined. FieldRT translates the retained geometric structure into a parameterized streaming architecture that shares field access and storage across spatial operators. The resulting custom-PL primitives achieve production-scale numerical closure on an AMD Kria KV260 and make otherwise infeasible multi-dilation execution realizable.

</details>


### [35] [Randomized Caches in Hardware: Challenges and Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00282)
*Xinrui Wang,Pascal Kosak,Christof Paar,Daniel Genkin,Yuval Yarom,Anirban Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 首个在统一全系统硬件平台上实现并对比四种随机化末级缓存（CEASER-S、ScatterCache、SassCache、MIRAGE）的工作：在乱序 RISC-V SoC 的 LLC 上完成 FPGA 实现，指出仅流水化 IDF 不足（其他组件成为频率瓶颈，降频达 35%），重构关键路径后可控制在基线 10% 以内，代价是延迟或并发度；IPC 退化主要来自访问延迟而非缺失率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机化缓存（randomized/encrypted-address cache）研究多停留在架构行为描述，缺乏可落地的硬件实现经验；且各设计在不同平台、不同仿真器下评测，无法公平比较其真实硬件代价（频率、延迟、面积）与端到端性能影响。

Method: 将四种代表性设计实现为多核乱序 RISC-V SoC 的 LLC，识别共性与设计特有的实现瓶颈并提出实际解决方案；仅流水化 index derivation function（IDF）不够，需对其他关键路径（映射、rekeying、victim selection 策略）做保持语义的重构；在 FPGA 上跑完 SPEC CPU 2017 rate 与 GAPBS 全负载，并与基线组相联缓存对比频率、延迟、LUT 用量，同时用 ASIC 综合评估面积。

Result: 四种设计中有三种 IDF 之外组件成为频率瓶颈，相对基线降频最高 35%；关键路径重构后三者降至基线频率的 10% 以内，但增加访问延迟或降低并发度（如 victim 选择/替换并行度）。IPC 退化由 LLC 访问延迟主导而非额外 miss，SPEC 最高 -5%，GAPBS 最高 -11%。开销随设计与工艺变化：FPGA LUT +10–35%，ASIC 面积 +1–14%。

Conclusion: 随机化缓存的真实代价瓶颈在关键路径与访问延迟而非命中率，架构仿真常低估其性能损失；实现层面的重构可显著收窄频率损失，但需在延迟/并发度上做显式权衡；提供了首个同平台可比的全系统硬件基线，对 LLM 推理等延迟敏感负载的 LLC 安全机制选型有直接参考价值。

Abstract: Randomized caches protect against side-channel attacks by obfuscating the mapping of memory addresses to cache locations. Existing proposals typically describe architectural behavior, leaving practical hardware implementation challenges largely unexplored. We implement four prominent designs, CEASER-S, ScatterCache, SassCache, and MIRAGE, and integrate them as the last-level cache (LLC) of a multi-core out-of-order RISC-V system-on-chip (SoC). We identify common and design-specific implementation challenges and develop practical solutions. We evaluate complete FPGA implementations by running SPEC CPU 2017 rate and GAPBS workloads to completion, providing, to our knowledge, the first comparative evaluation of these designs on a common full-system hardware platform. Pipelining the index derivation function (IDF) alone is insufficient: in three of the four variants, other components become the frequency bottleneck, reducing frequency by up to 35% relative to the baseline set-associative cache. Redesigning the critical paths while preserving mappings, rekeying, and victim-selection policies brings the three affected variants to no more than 10% below the baseline frequency, at the cost of added latency or reduced concurrency. Increased LLC access latency, rather than additional misses, dominates IPC degradation, reducing instructions per cycle by up to 5% for SPEC and 11% for GAPBS. Hardware overhead depends on the design and implementation technology: FPGA lookup-table usage increases by 10-35%, while ASIC area increases by 1-14%.

</details>


### [36] [BitLLM: Efficient Low-Bit, Conversion-Hiding LLM Decoding on Mobile GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00547)
*Hangyu Zheng,Zhihao Shu,Kunxiong Zhu,Minghai Qin,Xiaolong Ma,Gagan Agrawal,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: BitLLM 是面向移动 GPU 的低比特量化 LLM 推理引擎，核心贡献是 KV Cache 的自适应布局/打包策略与"整数原生"执行流水线（把类型转换推迟并移出计算热路径），在现成移动 GPU 上相对 LLAMA.CPP/MNN-LLM/LiteRT-LM/MLC-LLM 平均加速 1.78×/1.73×/1.58×/1.47×，长上下文生成时每 token 能耗最多降低 88.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端部署 LLM 受算力与内存限制，低比特量化（4-bit 权重、8-bit 激活）理论上可行，但实际在移动 GPU 上收益有限。作者定位到两个根因：(1) 量化值与浮点值之间频繁的 dtype 转换开销；(2) 自回归解码中不断增长的 KV Cache 导致的动态、低效访存模式（不规则长度、非对齐、cache line 利用率低）。

Method: 两项协同设计：(1) KV Cache 自适应 layout + packing 策略，使解码全程保持高效、连续、对齐的访存；(2) integer-native 执行流水线，将反量化/类型转换从 compute hot path 中延迟并重叠（与 MatMul、访存、非 MatMul 算子重叠），使关键 MatMul 阶段全程停留在整数域，避免 int↔fp 往返。工程实现上针对移动 GPU 的 warp/线程组、共享内存与 SIMD 打包特性做了 kernel 级优化。

Result: 在多种模型与多个量化位宽下，相对 LLAMA.CPP、MNN-LLM、LiteRT-LM、MLC-LLM 平均加速 1.78×、1.73×、1.58×、1.47×；长上下文生成场景下每 token 平均能耗最多降低 88.9%。

Conclusion: 量化 LLM 在移动端的主要瓶颈已从"算术精度/算力"转向"数据类型转换与访存效率"。通过 KV Cache 布局协同设计与整数域端到端流水，可在不牺牲精度的前提下取得显著延迟与能耗收益，说明移动端量化推理的优化重心应放在 memory layout 与 execution pipeline 的系统级协同，而非单点 kernel 加速。

Abstract: The rapid scaling of large language models (LLMs) has unlocked remarkable capabilities across various domains. However, deploying LLMs on mobile devices remains challenging because of their high computational and memory requirements. Low-bit quantization (e.g., 4-bit weights, 8-bit activations) can reduce model size while preserving accuracy, but its practical efficacy on mobile GPUs has been limited so far. In this paper, we identify that the performance gap stems primarily from frequent data type conversions between quantized and floating-point values, and dynamic and inefficient memory access patterns caused by the growing Key-Value (KV) cache during autoregressive decoding. We introduce BitLLM, a mobile inference engine that addresses type conversion overhead and suboptimal memory accesses in quantized LLMs. Our approach features an adaptive layout and packing strategy for the KV cache that ensures efficient memory access throughout LLM decoding. This is coupled with an integer-native execution pipeline that defers and overlaps type conversion outside the compute hot path, keeping the critical MatMul stages in the integer domain. Implemented on off-the-shelf mobile GPUs, BitLLM outperforms leading engines, i.e., LLAMA.CPP, MNN-LLM, LiteRT-LM, and MLC-LLM, achieving average speedups of 1.78$\times$, 1.73$\times$, 1.58$\times$, and 1.47$\times$, respectively, across various models and quantization levels. Under long-context generation, BitLLM reduces average energy consumption (per token) by up to 88.9%.

</details>


### [37] [TADA: Technology Aware DNN Accelerator Performance and Energy Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00292)
*Tanvi Sharma,Surya Selvam,Anand Raghunathan,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TADA 是一个"存储技术感知"的 DNN 加速器性能/能耗估计器：用"technology signature"（不同面积下读能量、写能量、存储容量的向量）把片上存储技术（SRAM/STT-MRAM/ReRAM/PCM/FeFET/eDRAM/CTT）编码为输入，配合 workload 与硬件配置直接预测系统延迟和能量，从而在 DSE 中免去每种新存储技术重做仿真+数据生成+模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘推理 DNN 加速器受能耗、延迟、面积（EDP）强约束，架构与 dataflow 需靠设计空间探索（DSE）确定。但现有 DSE 很少把片上存储技术当作一等公民：cell type、bank 组织、工艺节点通常被固定。存储容量增大可提升数据复用、减少访存，却带来面积与访问能耗上升，挤压计算单元预算，因此存储技术会从根本上改变给定面积/EDP 预算下的最优架构。已有 ML-based DSE 方法每换一种存储技术就要重复仿真、造数据、重新训练模型，成本不可扩展。

Method: 核心是把存储技术"参数化为输入"而非"绑定在模型/数据集里"。1) technology signature：用一组面积点上的读能量、写能量、存储容量构成向量，描述一种存储技术的面积—能耗权衡曲线；2) 合成数据集：覆盖现有与未来的技术趋势（21 条未见过的技术曲线之外还有训练覆盖范围），并施加基于物理的 guardrail（约束）以保证面积—能耗权衡的物理合理性；3) 模型以 workload + 硬件配置 + technology signature 为联合输入，直接回归系统延迟与能量，替代逐点 cycle-level/architectural 仿真。

Result: 在 21 条未见过的技术曲线（SRAM、STT-MRAM、ReRAM、PCM、FeFET、eDRAM、CTT）上，系统能量与延迟的平均绝对百分比误差分别为 1.5–4.4% 与 0.7–1.2%。三个 pairwise 案例研究中，在 98.7–100% 的面积预算点上，TADA 选出的存储技术与仿真器一致，平均 EDP 损失最多 0.21%，而评估吞吐提升超过 10^4 倍。

Conclusion: 把存储技术显式建模为可泛化的输入特征，是让 ML-based DSE 跨技术代际复用的可行路径。工程价值在于：新存储技术（尤其是 emerging NVM）出现时无需重跑仿真与重训模型，可把 DSE 的评估成本降低 4 个数量级以上，使"存储技术 × 架构 × dataflow"的联合探索、以及面向未来工艺的架构预研变得实用。局限在于精度仍依赖合成数据的仿真器保真度与物理 guardrail 的有效性，且目前只覆盖延迟/能量两个目标，面积与真实流片/硅后偏差未验证。

Abstract: Deep neural network (DNN) accelerators for edge inference must meet tight energy, latency, and area (EDP) constraints, and their optimal hardware architecture and dataflow configuration is typically identified through design space exploration (DSE). However, on-chip memory technology including cell type, bank organization, and process node, is rarely treated as a first-class citizen in DSE. For example, increasing memory capacity can reduce memory accesses through greater data reuse, but at the cost of higher area and access energy, thereby limiting the compute units budget. Consequently, memory technology can fundamentally shift the optimal accelerator architecture for a given area and EDP budget. Existing machine learning (ML)-based DSE methods require repeating simulation, data generation, and model training for each new memory technology. We present TADA, a memory technology-aware DNN accelerator performance and energy estimator that predicts system latency and energy for a given workload, hardware configuration, and memory technology. TADA is trained on a synthetic dataset that generalizes across memory technologies. The dataset spans current and future technology trends while enforcing physics-based guardrails to preserve realistic area-energy trade-offs. Each memory technology is represented by a technology signature, a vector of read energy, write energy, and storage capacity across a range of areas, provided as input alongside the workload and hardware configuration. Across 21 unseen technology curves spanning SRAM, STTRAM, ReRAM, PCM, FeFET, eDRAM, and CTT, TADA predicts system energy and latency with 1.5-4.4% and 0.7-1.2% mean absolute percentage error, respectively. In three pairwise case studies, it matches the simulator-selected memory technology at 98.7-100% of area budgets, with a mean EDP loss of at most 0.21%, while achieving over 10^4x higher evaluation throughput.

</details>


### [38] [Route-Aware EM Surrogate for Automated LC Matching Network Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00409)
*Subhadip Ghosh,Surya Srikar Peri,Ramesh Harjani,Sachin S. Sapatnekar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出开源自动化 LC 匹配网络优化框架，将原理图级设计优化与电磁(EM)感知的后版图精修结合；用 ML 代理模型预测单条走线的频变复数导纳，可在同一工艺节点下跨不同匹配网络拓扑与物理版图复用而无需重训练，并以之指导后版图无源器件重调，在保住版图面积的前提下恢复匹配性能。65nm CMOS 实测：代理相对全波 EM 求解器 MAPE 1.94–4.91%，无无源性违例；9 个 5–95GHz LC 匹配网络 S11 从后版图退化后的 -6.37dB 恢复到 EM 感知精修后的 -22.27dB，全版图 EM 验证全部达标，单次优化运行时间 <70s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RF/毫米波电路中 LC 匹配网络的设计通常先在原理图级用理想或无寄生元件优化，但版图引入的走线寄生、耦合与衬底效应会使 S11 显著退化（本文中退化到 -6.37dB），必须做后版图重调。传统的做法是每次版图变动都调用全波 EM 求解器（MoM/FEM）做迭代优化，单次仿真耗时数分钟到数小时，与优化循环所需的成百上千次评估不匹配；因此后版图 EM 感知优化在实践中往往被简化为手工调参或少样本试错，难以保证收敛到目标。此外，已有 ML-EM 代理工作大多针对某个特定拓扑或特定几何做端到端训练，换拓扑/换版图就得重新生成数据并重训练，数据成本和泛化性都限制了工程可用性。作者的动机是做出"一次训练、跨拓扑跨版图复用"的走线级代理，把 EM 感知优化真正嵌进自动化的设计闭环，并开源以可复现。

Method: 方法上分两个层次。第一层是原理图级优化：在理想/集总元件模型下做自动化的 LC 匹配网络拓扑与元件值搜索，得到满足目标的初始解。第二层是 EM 感知的后版图精修：对版图做参数化，把每条走线(route)视为独立的可学习对象，用 ML 代理模型预测其在目标频段内的频变复数导纳 Y(f)，从而在优化循环内以毫秒级代价替代全波求解。关键的建模选择是"走线级"而非"网络级"抽象——代理的输入是单条走线的几何/材料/工艺上下文，输出是它自身的导纳，因此同一工艺节点下的多条走线模型可像查表一样被任意拓扑和任意物理布局组合复用，拓扑或布局变化只改变如何把各走线导纳拼装成整体 S 参数，不触发重训练。代理预测的导纳再与集总无源元件模型联合组装成全网络响应，用于计算 S11 等指标并驱动无源器件(电容/电感/尺寸)的重调；优化目标中同时约束版图面积/占地不变，从而"保住 footprint 只调无源参数"。文中还提到对代理做了无源性(passivity)检查，确保预测的导纳不产生非物理的有源响应，这对把代理嵌入 S 参数级联与稳定性分析是必要的正确性保证。验证在商用 65nm CMOS 上做，最终以全版图全波 EM 仿真做独立校验。

Result: 在商用 65nm CMOS 工艺上：(1) 代理精度相对全波 EM 求解器为 MAPE 1.94–4.91%，且未检测到无源性违例；(2) 覆盖 5–95GHz 共 9 个 LC 匹配网络，原理图级优化结果经后版图寄生影响后退化到 S11 = -6.37dB，EM 感知后版图精修后恢复到 -22.27dB；(3) 所有设计在全版图 EM 验证下均满足匹配目标；(4) 单次优化运行时间低于 70s。需要指出的是，-6.37dB 与 -22.27dB 是这 9 个网络上的代表性/汇总指标（按摘要表述为跨网络取值），而非单一设计的逐步对比；另外 MAPE 是走线导纳层面的误差，摘要未给出该误差经网络组装与失配灵敏度放大后在 S11 上的端到端误差分布，这是评估可用裕度的关键缺口。

Conclusion: 该论文的系统贡献不是"用 ML 替代 EM"这一泛泛主张，而是把 ML-EM 代理的复用粒度下沉到"单条走线导纳"这一物理上可分解、工艺上稳定的层级，从而把此前"一拓扑一模型"的代理范式转成"一工艺节点一模型库、跨拓扑跨版图复用"，并据此把 EM 感知后版图优化从手工/少样本试错变成 70s 内可完成的自动闭环。对工程实践的意义在于：把昂贵的全波仿真从优化内循环移到外循环（仅做最终验证），使后版图重调成为可频繁执行的常规步骤，而非设计末期的救火；开源+可复现则降低了在其它节点（如 28nm/22nm FD-SOI、SiGe BiCMOS）上迁移的门槛。局限与后续方向：摘要未给出代理的训练数据规模/采样策略、导纳误差对 S11 的端到端敏感性、跨工艺角与温度/工艺偏差的鲁棒性，也未覆盖多端口耦合与相邻网络串扰（走线级分解最脆弱之处），以及扩展到更高频段（>100GHz）时的精度与失效率。总体看，这是一篇偏 EDA/设计自动化的系统与流程论文，对 AI 基础设施训练/推理栈的性能影响间接，但其"可复用、可组合、物理正确性受约束的廉价代理 + 昂贵求解器仅做校验"的模式，对芯片设计与硬件协同优化流水线是有借鉴价值的方法论。

Abstract: This paper proposes an open-source automated LC matching network optimization framework that combines schematic-level design optimization with electromagnetic (EM)-aware post-layout refinement. An ML-based surrogate predicts the frequency-dependent complex admittance of individual routes, enabling reuse across matching network topologies and physical layouts for the same technology node without retraining. These route models guide post-layout passive retuning to recover matching performance while preserving the layout footprint. Evaluated in a commercial 65nm CMOS technology, the surrogate achieves 1.94-4.91% mean absolute percentage error with respect to a full-wave electromagnetic solver, with no detected passivity violations. Across nine LC matching networks operating at 5-95GHz, our framework recovers S_11 from -6.37dB, for schematic-level optimization after post-layout degradation to -22.27dB after EM-aware post-layout refinement. All designs meet matching targets under full-layout EM verification in optimization runtimes below 70s.

</details>


### [39] [Efficient Reconfigurable Architectures for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00487)
*Endri Taka*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该博士论文面向异构 AI 加速器（AMD Versal AI Engines、Intel Stratix 10 NX Tensor Blocks、AMD Ryzen AI XDNA/XDNA2 NPU），系统优化稠密 GEMM，并提出支持结构化稀疏的 FPGA 内建硬核 SST。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习算力需求增长推动 AI 专用可重构器件出现，但其异构资源与新数据流模型难以高效利用；同时 FPGA 缺乏对结构化稀疏（2:4/1:4 等）的原生支持，导致稀疏模型难以获得实际加速收益。

Method: 1) MaxEVA：针对 Versal AIE 的 GEMM 映射与优化，并扩展至任意矩阵尺寸；2) 面向 Stratix 10 NX Tensor Block 的系统化 GEMM 优化框架；3) 提出 Systolic Sparse Tensor slice（SST）作为可综合/内建硬块，支持稠密、2:4、1:4 及新型 1:3 稀疏度；4) 将统一方法论迁移到 XDNA/XDNA2 NPU 进行 GEMM 优化。

Result: MaxEVA 相对 SOTA 吞吐最高 2.19×、能效提升 20.4%；Versal 与 Stratix 上分别达到 77 TOPS / 0.94 TOPS/W 与 68 TOPS / 1.35 TOPS/W（int8）。SST 相比常规 FPGA 逻辑频率最高 5×、面积低 10.9×；在稀疏 ViT/CNN 上相比稠密内建加速最高 3.52× 加速，面积仅增 13.3%。XDNA/XDNA2 上分别达到 6.76 / 38.05 TOPS（int8）与 3.14 / 14.71 TOPS（bf16）。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了从 AIE、Tensor Block 到 NPU 的一套跨器件 GEMM 优化方法论，并通过 SST 将结构化稀疏下沉到 FPGA 硬核层面，显著改善频率、面积与能效。

Abstract: The growing computational demands of deep learning (DL) have driven AI-optimized reconfigurable devices, such as the AMD Versal AI Engines (AIEs), the Intel Stratix 10 NX Tensor Blocks (TBs), and the AMD Ryzen AI NPUs. Their heterogeneous resources and novel dataflow models make efficient exploitation nontrivial, and FPGAs lack native support for structured sparsity. This dissertation optimizes dense general matrix multiplication (GEMM), the dominant DL kernel, on these devices, proposes FPGA architectural enhancements for structured sparsity, and extends the techniques to AMD Ryzen AI NPUs.
  First, MaxEVA optimizes GEMM on Versal AIEs, achieving up to 2.19$\times$ higher throughput and 20.4% higher energy efficiency than the state of the art. It is then extended to arbitrary matrix sizes, and a systematic framework is proposed for the Stratix 10 NX TBs. Both frameworks reach state-of-the-art int8 throughput of up to 77 and 68 TOPS, and energy efficiency of up to 0.94 and 1.35 TOPS/W, on Versal and Stratix, respectively.
  Next, Systolic Sparse Tensor slices (SSTs) are proposed as in-fabric hard blocks supporting dense, 2:4, 1:4, and a new 1:3 sparsity level. SST-based GEMM accelerators achieve up to 5$\times$ higher frequency and 10.9$\times$ lower area than conventional FPGA logic, and on sparse ViT and CNN models SSTs deliver up to 3.52$\times$ speedup over dense in-fabric acceleration with up to 13.3% area increase.
  Finally, a common systematic methodology is proposed to optimize GEMM across the XDNA and XDNA2 AMD NPUs, reaching state-of-the-art performance of up to 6.76 and 38.05 TOPS (int8) and 3.14 and 14.71 TOPS (bf16), respectively.

</details>


### [40] [A Mixed-Precision CNN-LSTM Accelerator Featuring Long-Kernel Memory Reuse and Dynamic Quantization for Sleep Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00660)
*Xinyu Chen,Yaqian Xu,Yuhan Hou,Bowen Liu,Andrew G. Richardson,Xilin Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一款面向EEG睡眠分期的CNN-LSTM混合精度加速器，采用蛇形（serpentine）数据流在相邻PE间传递激活值以降低长时序卷积的内存访问，结合运行时动态量化+逐层混合精度量化、位宽灵活MAC与通道分组存储调度，在65nm CMOS上实现0.40mW功耗、相对同频32b RISC-V达258倍加速，且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG睡眠分期属于超低功耗可穿戴/边缘场景的长时序深度学习负载：CNN-LSTM中存在较长的一维时序卷积，传统输出驻留/权重驻留数据流下激活值反复读写片上存储，访存成为主要能耗与带宽瓶颈；同时LSTM/MLP权重与激活分布动态范围差异大，统一位宽量化要么浪费位宽、要么损失精度，导致模型体积与能效难以同时最优。

Method: 1) 蛇形数据流：激活在相邻处理单元（PE）间逐级传递，配合长时序卷积的滑窗特性复用部分和，替代输出驻留/权重驻留的重复访存；2) 混合量化方案：LSTM与MLP层采用运行时动态量化（按运行时统计量确定scale），并逐层分配精度，CNN部分保留较高精度；3) 硬件支撑：位宽灵活（bit-flexible）MAC阵列支持INT2/4/8/16，配合通道分组（channel-grouped）存储调度以匹配不同层位宽与访存粒度；4) 65nm CMOS流片实现，并在MASS-SS3全部62名受试者上用31折leave-two-subject-out交叉验证评估5分类精度与N3敏感度。

Result: 相比输出驻留与权重驻留数据流，访存分别降低6.2倍与2.25倍；模型体积缩小4.16倍；功耗0.40mW，相对同频32b RISC-V处理器加速258倍；在MASS-SS3全部62名受试者、31折leave-two-subject-out验证下，面向通用睡眠分期的混合量化方案5分类精度与浮点基线差距在0.15%以内，面向深睡眠检测优化时N3敏感度差距在0.51%以内。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：面向长时序CNN-LSTM的边缘推理，"数据流重用（PE间激活传递）+ 逐层混合精度与动态量化 + 位宽灵活MAC"三者协同可在近乎无损精度下同时获得数量级的访存、模型体积与能效收益；所提技术对EEG类时序深度学习负载具备通用性，但其结论主要基于单一数据集与低功耗小规模电路，向更大模型/多模态生理信号扩展时的编译调度与量化误差累积仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: This letter presents a mixed-precision CNN-LSTM accelerator for EEG-based sleep staging. To efficiently execute long temporal convolutions, a serpentine dataflow propagates activations between neighboring processing elements, reducing memory accesses by 6.2 times and 2.25 times compared with output- and weight-stationary dataflows, respectively. A hybrid quantization scheme applies runtime dynamic quantization to the LSTM and MLP layers and assigns mixed precision on a layer-by-layer basis, reducing model size by 4.16 times. Combined with a bit-flexible MAC architecture and channel-grouped memory scheduling, the accelerator supports INT2/4/8/16 inference. Fabricated in 65 nm CMOS, the accelerator consumes 0.40 mW and achieves a 258 times speedup over an iso-frequency 32b RISC-V processor. Evaluated across all 62 subjects in the public MASS-SS3 dataset using 31-fold leave-two-subject-out cross-validation, the mixed-precision approach with hybrid quantization maintains 5-class accuracy within 0.15% of the floating-point baseline when optimized for general sleep staging and N3 sensitivity within 0.51% when optimized for deep-sleep detection. The developed techniques are broadly applicable to EEG-based DL workloads.

</details>


### [41] [QFabric: Guarded Cross-Domain Task Recovery and Its Limits on a Linux-RTOS Board](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01598)
*Ayushman Bhattacharya,Anwesha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 在异构嵌入式板（Linux AP + 实时 MCU）上，将“跨域迁移”形式化为对经验软实时契约违约的带守卫恢复：任务双域编译、滑窗迟滞监控、静默纪元边界处做语义与容量准入、试运行后提交或回滚。原型 QFabric 在 Arduino UNO Q（A53+Zephyr M33）上实测表明：Bridge RPC 每次调用约 19 ms 固定调度开销、MCU 跑最重内核慢 34x、且因每次调用都穿越 Linux 用户态，Linux 侧争用会把 MCU 路径放大到 p99 400 ms（Linux 路径 17.0 ms）；因此迁移无法让任务免受 Linux 干扰，守卫的价值主要在于阻止有害迁移（无迟滞控制器在 7/30 格迁移并使 p95 最坏放大 177x，试运行回滚了其中 5 次）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构嵌入式板天然提供“Linux 应用处理器 + 实时 MCU”两个执行域，直觉上应在任务时序退化时把任务搬到另一域。但要判断这种迁移是否真能提供实时性隔离，必须先把跨域放置的失败模式量化：RPC 固定开销、MCU 相对算力、以及 Linux 侧争用对 MCU 路径的放大效应。作者还指出早期工件release报告的改进来自测量缺陷，需要重新建立可信的测量与判定方法。

Method: 把跨域放置建模为“受守卫的恢复”：同一有界任务编译到两个域；按窗口＋迟滞监控违约；仅在静默纪元边界（quiescent epoch boundary）做语义兼容与容量准入后切换；切换后经 probation 期再提交或回滚。系统侧实现完整测量栈：刻画 stock Bridge RPC 互连，在 idle / CPU-load / 高优先级 SCHED_FIFO 争用三种条件下，用普通与实时调用者测量五个工作负载在两个域的表现；并在四节点链的 30 个单元上对比“带迟滞 QFabric”与“无迟滞控制器”。同时公开源码、原始 trace 与生成器，并指出并修正前版工件的测量缺陷。

Result: 每次 MCU 调用约 19 ms 调度开销，与计算量无关；MCU 上最重内核比 Linux 慢 34x；由于每调用必穿 Linux 用户态，Linux 争用对 MCU 路径的放大远大于 Linux 路径（p99 达 400 ms vs 17.0 ms）。30 个单元中 Linux 满足全部契约、miss 率 ≤0.8%，QFabric 正确地从不迁移；无迟滞控制器在 7 个单元发生迁移，使 p95 最坏上升 177x，probation 回滚了其中 5 次迁移。

Conclusion: 在由 Linux 分发调用的异构板上，跨域迁移无法把任务与 Linux 干扰隔离，反而常常引入有害迁移；恢复守卫（迟滞＋准入＋试运行回滚）的主要工程价值是抑制这类有害迁移，而非提升尾部延迟。该结论对“MCU 作为实时卸载域”的设计直觉构成明确反证，并强调互连与调用路径（用户态穿越）才是决定跨域实时性的关键，同时凸显可复现测量与误差审计的重要性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous embedded boards pair a Linux application processor with a real-time microcontroller (MCU), which suggests moving a task between the two domains when its timing degrades. We present QFabric, a prototype that treats cross-domain placement as guarded recovery from a violated empirical soft real-time contract: a bounded task is compiled for both domains, monitored in windows with hysteresis, switched only after semantic and capacity admission at a quiescent epoch boundary, and committed or rolled back after probation. On an Arduino UNO Q (Cortex-A53 Linux and a Cortex-M33 running Zephyr), we characterize the stock Bridge RPC interconnect and measure five workloads in both domains under idle, CPU-load, and higher-priority SCHED_FIFO contention, with ordinary and real-time callers. Each MCU invocation costs about 19 ms of dispatch regardless of compute, the MCU runs the heaviest kernel 34x slower, and because every call traverses Linux userspace, Linux contention inflates the MCU path far more than the Linux path (p99 up to 400 ms versus 17.0 ms). Across 30 cells of a four-node chain, Linux meets every contract within 0.8% misses and QFabric correctly never migrates; a controller without hysteresis migrates in 7 cells, raising p95 by up to 177x, and probation rolls back 5 of those moves. On Linux-dispatched heterogeneous boards, migration therefore cannot isolate a task from Linux interference, and recovery guards matter chiefly by preventing harmful moves. This paper supersedes an earlier artifact release whose reported improvement arose from measurement defects that we identify. All source, raw traces, and generators are public.

</details>


### [42] [FPGA Acceleration of CNN-BiLSTM for EEG-Based Sleep Staging Featuring FFT-Based Non-Unit Stride Fast Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00669)
*Aaron Zhou,Yaqian Xu,Chen Chen,James Yen,Weihang Zheng,Mark Zhao,Mingzhe Sun,Naize Yang,Andrew G. Richardson,Xilin Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一款面向可穿戴实时睡眠分期的FPGA CNN-BiLSTM加速器，通过FFT-based非单位步长快速卷积、全流水线2的幂量化、块卷积、深度优先执行与硬件引擎复用等软硬件协同设计，在Xilinx Zynq-7000上以10 MHz、11 mW动态功耗、仅4个DSP实现长核时序卷积的高效边缘推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时可穿戴睡眠分期模型依赖长核时间卷积（long-kernel temporal convolution），其算术量与访存开销大，现有加速器难以高效支持；同时边缘设备受功耗、资源与存储严格约束，需要在保持精度的前提下大幅削减计算与访存。

Method: 算法-硬件协同设计：(1) FFT-based非单位步长快速卷积，在频域做累加，使每个block只需一次IFFT，后处理复杂度降至O(N log N)，在目标长核卷积层减少20.5×算术操作；(2) 全流水线（含FFT卷积与BiLSTM激活）采用2的幂量化（power-of-two quantization）；(3) 块卷积(block convolution)、深度优先执行(depth-first execution)、硬件引擎复用以减少访存、提高资源利用率；(4) 在Xilinx Zynq-7000 FPGA上实现，由MCU负责数据搬运与配置。

Result: 长核卷积层算术操作减少20.5×；在MASS-SS3公开数据集上所有受试者平均精度仅下降0.11%（相对浮点基线）；加速器运行于10 MHz，动态功耗11 mW（含数据搬运与MCU配置开销），仅用4个DSP，DSP能效达0.91 GOPS/W/DSP。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过频域快速卷积+低比特幂量化+调度与复用优化，可在极低功耗、极少DSP资源下支撑长核时序模型的实时睡眠分期推理，具备向其他可穿戴应用推广的可扩展性。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) models for real-time wearable sleep staging often rely on long-kernel temporal convolutions, a requirement that current accelerators struggle to support efficiently. To overcome this, we present an FPGA-based CNN-BiLSTM accelerator that incorporates several algorithm-hardware co-design techniques to enable efficient edge inference. Central to this design is an FFT-based non-unit stride fast convolution, which performs accumulation in the frequency domain to enable a single IFFT per block, reducing post-processing complexity to $O(N\log N)$. On the target long-kernel convolution layer, this yields a 20.5$\times$ reduction in arithmetic operations. Power-of-two quantization is applied across the pipeline, including FFT-based convolution and BiLSTM activations, with an average accuracy degradation of only 0.11% relative to the floating-point baseline across all subjects on the public MASS-SS3 dataset. Additional optimizations, including block convolution, depth-first execution, and hardware engine reuse minimize memory access and enhance resource utilization. The accelerator, implemented on a Xilinx Zynq-7000 FPGA, operates at 10 MHz and consumes 11 mW of dynamic power including data handling and configuration overhead from a microcontroller. The design achieves a DSP power efficiency of 0.91 GOPS/W/DSP using only 4 DSPs, demonstrating scalable and energy-efficient DL acceleration for sleep staging that can be extended to other wearable applications.

</details>


### [43] [Elastic Precision: Software-Hardware Co-Design of a Mixed-Precision Architecture for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00915)
*Boyang Chen,Xiao Wu,Haoran Wu,Yiren Zhao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Coffee 是一种面向长上下文 LLM 推理的运行时混合精度分配架构：在固定平均位宽预算下，用当前 GEMM 分块的两个输入矩阵的统计信息联合估计候选位宽下的输出误差，为每个子阵列选取两个输入的位宽；配套硬件支持非均匀位宽的数据访问与计算，并将误差估计/位宽选择与数据传输和计算流水重叠；同一读取通路还支持解码阶段的 attention-guided KV 读取以减少访存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低位量化可降低 LLM 推理的搬运与算力开销，但统一位宽会浪费精度并损害模型质量。现有混合精度方法在选择某个输入（如权重）位宽时，未充分考虑当前 GEMM tile 的另一个输入（如激活）对输出误差的影响；而同时利用两个输入来指导精度选择，又会带来额外的估计与选择开销，在常规硬件上难以摊薄。因此需要一个在固定平均位宽预算下、低开销的运行时双输入感知精度分配方案与相应硬件结构。

Method: 1) 误差模型：对每个 GEMM 子阵列，结合两个当前输入矩阵的统计量，估计在各候选位宽组合下的输出误差（而非仅依据单侧输入的量化误差）。2) 分配策略：在固定平均位宽约束下，按“估计误差下降/位宽”的边际收益做贪心/定制化搜索，为每个子阵列选定两个输入各自的位宽。3) 硬件：设计支持非均匀位宽的数据访问通路与计算单元（可变位宽乘法/累加、位宽感知的打包与读取）。4) 流水化：将误差估计与位宽选择和数据搬运、计算重叠，使选择开销不进入关键路径，限制停顿。5) 复用读取通路实现解码期 attention-guided KV 读取（按注意力重要性裁剪/稀疏化 KV 读取），降低长上下文解码的显存带宽压力。

Result: 在量化基线对比中取得更低 perplexity 与更高平均 zero-shot 准确率；长上下文检索召回率优于 PLENA。对长上下文解码的系统建模显示：相对重新配置的 PLENA 基线，吞吐最高 2.55×、能效最高 2.61×；相对 B200 GPU 基线，吞吐最高 4.96×、能效最高 12.56×。

Conclusion: Coffee 表明“双输入感知 + 固定平均位宽预算 + 运行时精细分配 + 选择与计算重叠”是一条可行的低开销量化路线，且精度分配硬件的数据通路可与注意力引导的 KV 读取复用，兼顾精度与访存效率。需注意其收益主要来自系统建模与架构级评估（含与重新配置的 PLENA 及 B200 的对比），实际加速比取决于位宽搜索开销能否被充分隐藏、非均匀位宽的硬件效率以及长上下文负载的注意力稀疏度。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization reduces the data movement and computation costs of large language model (LLM) inference, but uniform widths can waste precision and degrade model quality. Existing mixed-precision methods do not fully account for how the other input of the current GEMM tile affects output error when selecting one input's width. Using both inputs to guide precision introduces additional estimation and selection overheads on conventional hardware. We propose Coffee, a runtime precision allocation architecture for long-context LLM inference. Under a fixed average bit-width budget, Coffee combines statistics from both current input matrices to estimate output errors at candidate widths and select each subarray's two input widths, allocating more bits where they yield greater estimated error reductions. Its mixed-precision hardware supports the selected widths in data access and computation, while overlapping error estimation and width selection with transfers and computation to limit stalls. The same read path supports attention-guided KV reads during decoding, reducing memory traffic. Coffee achieves lower perplexity and higher average zero-shot accuracy than the evaluated quantization baselines, and higher long-context retrieval recall than PLENA. For long-context decoding, system modeling projects up to $2.55\times$ the throughput and $2.61\times$ the energy efficiency of the reconfigured PLENA baseline, and up to $4.96\times$ and $12.56\times$, respectively, compared with the B200 GPU baseline.

</details>


### [44] [A Surrogate Model for Variation-Aware Clock-Mesh Analysis and Design-Space Exploration using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01473)
*Muhammad Hadir Khan,Wajid Ali,Arya Thottiparambil Manikandan,Matthew Guthaus*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出时钟网格(clock mesh)的GNN代理模型，用新图表示与新架构同时预测功耗、sink端延迟/slew及OCV下的skew直方图；对比SPICE误差3.40ps延迟、4.14ps slew、4.46ps skew、功耗误差3.4%，OCV建模下较近似方法加速18x–1706x；案例研究找到缓冲器候选，99分位skew改善49.1%、功耗降25.7%、面积降37.3%，代价是99分位最大slew增6.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时钟网格因reconvergent path与多源驱动形成环，STA无法处理，工业界只能依赖SPICE，精度高但极慢。因此时钟网格综合只能用近似模型，而近似模型忽略了输入slew与输入skew导致的非线性缓冲器驱动等关键效应，导致延迟/skew估计偏差大（如74.37ps延迟、27.51ps skew），无法支撑有效的设计空间探索。目标是构造既准确又快的SPICE级代理模型。

Method: 以SPICE数据训练的图神经网络代理模型：（1）新的图表示编码时钟网格的多源驱动与重汇聚拓扑；（2）新架构联合预测sink延迟、slew与网格功耗；（3）支持OCV下的skew直方图预测，而非仅标称值；（4）在未见过的真实设计上做泛化测试，并与先前模型及当前SOTA近似方法对比；最后把代理模型嵌入综合流程做buffering候选搜索的案例研究。

Result: 未见设计上平均误差：延迟3.40ps、slew 4.14ps、skew 4.46ps（先前模型为6.28/12.35/11.62ps，SOTA近似方法为74.37ps延迟与27.51ps skew）；功耗平均误差3.4%；OCV建模下相对近似方法加速18x–1706x。案例研究：99分位skew改善49.1%、功耗降25.7%、面积降37.3%，代价为99分位最大slew增6.8%。

Conclusion: GNN代理模型在精度上接近SPICE（ps量级误差）、在速度上远超近似模型，使OCV感知的时钟网格设计空间探索与自动buffering优化变得可行；其意义在于把原本必须SPICE-in-the-loop的网格综合问题转为可微/可快速评估的代理优化，属于EDA/物理设计流程中的surrogate加速贡献，而非训练/推理系统层面的贡献。

Abstract: Clock meshes are widely used in high-performance VLSI systems to minimize skew and tolerate On-Chip Variation (OCV) in nanometer technologies. However, analyzing meshes is difficult because reconvergent paths and multi-source drivers prevent static timing analysis (STA) tools from handling mesh loops, making SPICE the industrial standard. SPICE simulations are accurate but slow, so clock-mesh synthesis relies on approximate models that overlook critical effects such as non-linear buffer driving caused by input slew and input skew. We propose a Graph Neural Network (GNN) based surrogate model of the clock mesh with a new graph representation and a new architecture that predicts power while also improving the delay and slew predictions at the sinks and capturing the skew histogram under OCV. Trained on SPICE data and tested on unseen real designs, our model achieves average errors of $3.40$~ps in delay, $4.14$~ps in slew and $4.46$~ps in skew, compared with $6.28$~ps, $12.35$~ps, and $11.62$~ps for our prior model, and $74.37$~ps delay and $27.51$~ps skew for the approximate method used in current state-of-the-art works. Our model predicts power within $3.4\%$ of SPICE on average, while delivering $18\times$-$1706\times$ speed-ups over the approximate method under OCV modeling, enabling faster and more accurate clock-mesh design-space exploration. A case study on a real design further shows that our surrogate model finds buffering candidates that, at a balanced tradeoff, improves $99^{\text{th}}$-percentile skew by $49.1\%$, reduces power by $25.7\%$, and reduces area by $37.3\%$ at the cost of a $6.8\%$ increase in $99^{\text{th}}$-percentile maximum slew as compared to the state-of-the-art work.

</details>


### [45] [SparseForge: Beyond Spike Sparsity toward Hardware-Exploitable Neuron Pruning for Efficient SNN Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01611)
*Kainan Wang,Sanhe Fu,Aili Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SparseForge 是一个软硬件协同的稀疏 SNN 加速框架，通过 G-LIF 可学习神经元剪枝、双正则化、稀疏 CWP 数据流和 mask-aware SPU 微架构，将「输出神经元冗余」带来的状态维护、psum 访问与片上存储开销一并消除，FPGA 上达到 296.94–619.49 GOPS/W。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN 的事件驱动与操作级稀疏（spike sparsity）只能减少乘累加次数，却无法消除冗余输出神经元导致的固定开销：神经元状态（膜电位）维护、psum 读写带宽、片上 buffer 容量。即稀疏性发生在「操作维度」，而成本瓶颈在「神经元维度」，二者不匹配，导致实际能效提升远低于理论稀疏率。

Method: 三层协同：(1) 算法层：提出 gated LIF（G-LIF），在保持二值 spike 语义的前提下学习可微神经元 mask；设计双正则化，同时对齐突触操作数削减与 psum buffer 容量削减（容量感知正则）。(2) 数据流层：稀疏 Column-Wise Product（CWP）数据流，配合增量式 spike 编码变体，提升权重访问规整性，并在动态 spike 活动下压缩稀疏索引开销。(3) 架构层：mask-aware 稀疏处理单元 SPU，支持压缩 spike 解码、稀疏隐式 im2col、mask 门控的 psum 更新以及解耦的稀疏执行。

Result: 在多种 SNN backbone 与数据集上保留 1.75%–77.99% 神经元、0.16%–62.36% 突触操作，精度-效率可配置；容量感知正则最高降低 psum buffer 需求 96.18%。Xilinx ZCU9EG FPGA 实现达 296.94–619.49 GOPS/W；在匹配网络与数据集条件下，相比代表性 FPGA SNN 加速器 FPS 高 1.24–4.17 倍、GOPS/W 高 1.50–7.18 倍、EDP 降低 1.70–19.13 倍。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：只有把剪枝目标从「操作稀疏」扩展到「神经元与 buffer 容量稀疏」，并让数据流与微架构显式感知 mask，才能把算法稀疏率转化为端到端硬件收益（带宽、存储、EDP）；其为边缘 SNN 加速器提供了可配置的精度-效率旋钮，方法学上可迁移到其他动态稀疏/事件驱动加速器设计。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) provide an event-driven paradigm for energy-efficient edge computing. However, operation-level sparsity in SNNs does not necessarily eliminate the hardware costs associated with redundant output neurons, including neuron-state maintenance, partial-sum (psum) accesses, and on-chip storage requirements. This paper presents SparseForge, a hardware-aware sparse SNN acceleration framework that jointly optimizes neuron pruning, sparse dataflow, and accelerator microarchitecture. At the algorithm level, we introduce a gated leaky integrate-and-fire (G-LIF) neuron to learn differentiable neuron masks with binary spike semantics. A dual regularization scheme is further developed to align pruning with both synaptic-operation reduction and psum buffer capacity reduction. At the dataflow level, we design a sparse Column-Wise Product (CWP) dataflow with a variant of incremental spike encoding to improve weight-access regularity and reduce sparse-index overhead under dynamic spike activity. At the architecture level, we develop a mask-aware sparse processing unit (SPU) that supports compressed spike decoding, sparse implicit im2col conversion, neuron-mask-gated psum updates, and decoupled sparse execution. Experimental results across multiple SNN backbones and datasets show that SparseForge retains 1.75%--77.99% of neurons and 0.16%--62.36% of synaptic operations, with a configurable accuracy--efficiency trade-off. The buffer-capacity-aware regularization reduces the required psum buffer capacity by up to 96.18\%. FPGA implementations on a Xilinx ZCU9EG achieve 296.94--619.49 GOPS/W across the evaluated workloads. Across matched network and dataset settings, SparseForge provides 1.24--4.17 higher FPS and 1.50--7.18 higher GOPS/W than representative FPGA-based SNN accelerators, while reducing EDP by 1.70--19.13, with benchmark-dependent accuracy--efficiency trade-offs.

</details>


### [46] [SMART-AIMC: Sensitivity-Aware Mixed ADC Resolution with Fine-Grained Tunability for Analog In-Memory Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01707)
*Yung-Chin Chen,Ourania Stouraiti,Naveen Verma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SMART-AIMC 提出一种"ADC 通道敏感度（ACS）"指标，把算法任务损失与模拟存内计算（IMC）的输出端 ADC 量化噪声关联起来，并配合无运行时开销的通道重排，在硬件粒度约束下做细粒度的混合 ADC 分辨率分配，从而在精度-能耗上取得更优的 Pareto 前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IMC 在模拟域内完成 GEMM，能显著提升能效与吞吐，但其量化噪声发生在 GEMM 输出端（ADC 量化），与常见的权重/激活量化（发生在输入侧）在误差传播机制上不同，会导致明显的任务精度下降。混合 ADC 分辨率（高/低分辨率模式）可提供精度-能耗折中，但如何在 IMC 单元间分配分辨率、并同时满足硬件粒度约束（同一 ADC 共享通道/单元只能取统一模式）是一个难点：粗粒度约束会破坏细粒度的敏感度分配收益。

Method: 1) 提出 ACS 指标，将各 ADC 通道的输出量化误差对最终任务 loss 的影响显式建模（即 loss 对该通道量化噪声的敏感度），使算法层精度与硬件层噪声建立可计算的映射；2) 基于 ACS 做系统化、有纪律的资源分配——按敏感度排序，把高分辨率 ADC 优先分配给关键计算；3) 提出基于敏感度的通道重排（channel reordering），把敏感度相近的通道聚集到同一硬件粒度组内，从而绕开粒度约束、逼近模型可达的最优 ADC 量化，且重排为离线/静态映射，无运行时开销。

Result: 在多种负载上评估，SMART-AIMC 给出了更优的精度-能耗 Pareto 前沿：(1) 在相同能耗预算下取得更高任务精度；(2) 支持跨越效率目标的细粒度调优，从激进节能到接近数字推理的精度。在后一个工作点上，线性投影相比 INT8 数字基线获得 6.99× 的建模系统级能效提升，且精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 该工作把 IMC 的输出端 ADC 量化误差与任务损失显式桥接，用敏感度驱动的混合分辨率分配 + 零开销通道重排解决硬件粒度约束，使 IMC 在接近数字推理精度时仍保有约 7× 能效优势；其核心贡献在于把"硬件噪声参数选择"从经验调参转为可计算的敏感度优化问题，并可推广到其他受粗粒度资源约束的近似计算/量化系统。

Abstract: Analog In-Memory Computing (IMC) is a promising paradigm for accelerating GEMM-based AI workloads by executing computations directly within the memory array in the analog domain. While IMC delivers substantial energy and throughput improvements, it can suffer from significant task accuracy degradation due to analog-to-digital converter (ADC) quantization noise, which occurs at the GEMM output and is thus different from weight/activation quantization, which occurs at the GEMM input. Mixed ADC resolution allows IMC modules to choose between high- and low-resolution ADC modes with different energy costs, offering a compelling accuracy-energy trade-off. However, selecting ADC resolutions across IMC units while respecting hardware granularity constraints remains challenging. In this paper, we propose SMART-AIMC, a Sensitivity-aware Mixed ADC Resolution framework with fine-grained Tunability for Analog In-Memory Computing. SMART-AIMC correlates the algorithmic task loss to hardware ADC quantization noise in the form of an ADC Channel Sensitivity (ACS) metric. Using this metric, SMART-AIMC systematically, and in a disciplined manner, allocates ADC resources to critical computations. Furthermore, we introduce a sensitivity-based channel reordering scheme that overcomes the hardware granularity constraints with no runtime overhead and approaches the optimal ADC quantization possible for the model. Evaluated across diverse workloads, SMART-AIMC unlocks a superior accuracy-energy Pareto frontier, providing (1) higher task accuracy at matched energy budgets and (2) fine-grained tuning across efficiency targets, from aggressive energy savings to near-digital inference accuracy. At the latter operating point, SMART-AIMC achieves $6.99\times$ higher modeled system-level energy efficiency for linear projections than the INT8 digital baseline with negligible accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [47] [Superscalar Loop Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01904)
*Luca Colagrande,Pascal Etterli,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Superscalar Loop Processor (SLP) 架构，利用循环内核的静态结构实现轻量级多发射执行，避免动态乱序调度的开销；并给出开源原型 Schnizo（基于带浮点的小型 RV32I 核），在 ML/HPC 微内核上大幅提升 IPC 与 FPU 利用率，同时保持顺序设计的能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程加速器为提升集成密度与能效，普遍采用大量单发射顺序（in-order）PE 阵列，导致每 PE 的 IPC 上限为 1，功能单元（FU）利用率低。传统超标量通过提高 IPC 改善 FU 利用率，但面积与能耗代价大，不适合 PE 阵列这种"小而多"的场景。

Method: 提出 SLP 执行模型：针对规则、计算密集型循环内核，利用其静态结构（可静态分析的循环迭代与指令依赖）实现多发射，而无需动态乱序调度逻辑。并实现开源原型 Schnizo，在支持浮点的小型 RV32I 核上扩展为超标量循环处理器。

Result: 在多个 ML 与 HPC 微内核上，Schnizo 相比标量基线取得显著更高的 IPC 与 FPU 利用率，同时仍保持轻量顺序设计的高能效特性。

Conclusion: SLP 为面向规则计算密集型负载的可编程加速器提供了一种高效架构方案：以远低于传统超标量的复杂度换取接近超标量的发射能力，适合作为 PE 阵列的核微架构。

Abstract: To sustain the computational demands of modern ML and HPC workloads, programmable accelerators integrate large arrays of processing elements (PEs), typically built around single-issue in-order cores to maximize integration density and energy efficiency. As a result, each PE's instruction throughput is upper-bounded at one instruction per cycle (IPC), often suffering from significant underutilization of its functional units (FUs). Conventional superscalar processors can improve FU utilization by increasing IPC, but they typically incur substantial area and energy overheads. This work proposes the Superscalar Loop Processor (SLP) architecture, a lightweight approach to superscalar execution that targets regular, compute-intensive loop kernels. We present the SLP execution model, which exploits the static structure of these workloads to enable efficient multiple-issue execution without the complexity of dynamic out-of-order scheduling. We demonstrate the SLP architecture through an open-source prototype implementation, Schnizo, developed as an enhancement of a tiny RV32I core with floating-point support. Across several ML and HPC microkernels, Schnizo achieves substantially higher IPC and FPU utilization than the scalar baseline while retaining the efficiency of lightweight in-order designs. These results demonstrate that SLPs can provide an efficient architectural solution for programmable accelerators targeting regular, compute-intensive workloads.

</details>


### [48] [Naviq: An Open-Source System-on-Chip Simulator for Rapid Prototyping, Verification, and Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02023)
*Scott Smith,Lukas Zscherpel,Alyssa Huang,Marissa Lanz,Deming Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Naviq 是一个面向 AMD Versal 级 FPGA SoC 的开源、周期级全系统软件仿真器：在 C++ 中建模硬化 SoC 基础设施（处理器、AXI4 NoC、存储子系统），通过 Verilator 集成自定义加速器，精度接近 Vivado RTL NoC 模型但在所评估场景下加速 81x–182x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 FPGA SoC 的端到端性能由可编程逻辑、硬化处理器、NoC、存储等组件的交互共同决定，单独评估某个加速器已不足。现有手段存在两难：厂商 RTL 仿真能捕捉交互但太慢，无法支撑迭代式设计空间探索；通用软件模型速度可接受但对决定真实行为的硬件过度抽象，精度不足。因此需要一个既准确又足够快的全系统仿真器用于调试与 DSE。

Method: 采用"分层保真"的混合仿真架构：(1) 硬化 SoC 基础设施（CPU、存储、NoC）用 C++ 周期级建模，以获得吞吐；(2) 自定义加速器经 Verilator 编译为 C++ 模型接入，保留 RTL 语义；(3) 核心是一个符合 AXI4 的 NoC 模型，显式刻画事务排序、packetization、路由、反压与访存交互；(4) 提供协议检查、checkpointing、tracing 与性能分析工具链，支撑调试和设计空间探索。

Result: 与 Vivado 的 RTL NoC 模型相比，Naviq 能较好复现带宽与延迟特性，同时在所评估的仿真中实现 81x–182x 加速；论文通过五个案例研究展示其在全系统评估、调试与 DSE 上的可用性。

Conclusion: Naviq 填补了"准确但慢的厂商仿真"与"快但过度抽象的软件模型"之间的空白，使现代 FPGA SoC 的全系统评估在工程上变得可行，对异构 SoC 的架构探索、软硬件协同调试具有直接实用价值。

Abstract: Modern FPGA SoCs are no longer just collections of accelerators on top of a programmable fabric. They combine programmable logic with hardened processors, NoCs, and components that dominate end-to-end performance. Evaluating individual pieces is insufficient; system performance depends on the interplay of SoC components. However, current FPGA SoC simulations provide limited support for this style of evaluation. Vendor simulations capture these interactions but are prohibitively slow for iterative design-space exploration. At the same time, generic software models overly abstract the hardware components that determine real behavior, resulting in less accurate simulations. Fast, easy-to-use simulators that accurately model real SoCs are critical for system debugging and design space exploration.
  We present Naviq, an open-source, cycle-level software simulator for FPGA SoCs, tuned to AMD Versal-class architectures. Naviq bridges accurate but slow vendor simulation and fast software modeling: it captures key behaviors of modern commercial FPGA SoCs while being fast enough for debugging and design-space exploration. Naviq models hardened SoC infrastructure in C++ and integrates accelerators through Verilator, allowing designers to evaluate custom hardware in a full-system environment. At the center of Naviq is an AXI4-compliant NoC model that captures transaction ordering, packetization, routing, backpressure, and memory interactions. Naviq provides CPU and memory integration, protocol checking, checkpointing, tracing, and performance-analysis tools for debugging and design-space exploration. Experimental results show Naviq closely reproduces the bandwidth and latency of Vivado's RTL NoC model while accelerating the evaluated simulations by 81x-182x. We present five case studies demonstrating Naviq's capabilities. Naviq enables practical full-system evaluation of modern FPGA SoC designs.

</details>


### [49] [SCARF: A Scene-Adaptive Depth-Guided G-3DGS Encoder Accelerator with Semantic Reuse and Fused Dataflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02365)
*Zirui Ma,Zhihua Fan,Haibin Wu,Wenxing Li,Fulin Zhang,Dongrui Fan,Xiaochun Ye,Wenming Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SCARF 是一个面向可泛化 3D Gaussian Splatting（G-3DGS）前馈编码器的数据流协同设计加速器。它把两个主要瓶颈——逐像素穷举深度搜索（占 65%+ 访存流量）和深度预测→高斯生成串行流水导致的冗余高斯（>52%）——分别映射为特征空间语义局部性和 tile 级场景自适应稀疏化，从而在 28nm 27.8mm² 后布局设计上取得 2.94x 几何平均端到端加速、PSNR 下降 <0.072%、2.23 inf/J 与 34.5x GPU 能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: G-3DGS 无需逐场景优化即可从多视图图像直接重建高斯原语，适合边缘端前馈新视角合成，但共享编码器存在两个结构性瓶颈：(1) cost-volume 的逐像素深度搜索忽略 2D 特征空间的语义相似性，导致冗余穷举候选扫描占访存流量 65% 以上，是典型的访存带宽墙而非算力墙问题；(2) "先深度预测、再高斯生成"的串行流水忽略局部 3D 几何连续性，使 52% 以上的高斯在实例化前即为冗余，属于无效计算与无效数据搬运。现有 GPU/通用加速器无法利用这两类场景级规律。

Method: 两项数据流与微架构协同设计：(1) Feature Similarity Depth Reuse（特征相似度深度复用）——将深度搜索重构为特征空间语义局部性问题，用特征亲和度而非物理地址邻近性判定深度一致性，把逐像素穷举扫描转为以 anchor 为中心的局部搜索，从而裁剪 cost-volume 的候选假设与相关访存。(2) Scene-Adaptive Early Sparsification（场景自适应早期稀疏化）——打破"深度→材质化"的串行边界，按 tile 探测局部场景规律，仅激活最小充分的高斯生成通路，在原语实例化之前消除冗余高斯计算。二者共同构成从算法规约到 PE/存储层次与片上缓冲的数据流重映射。

Result: 28nm、27.8 mm² 后布局实现：端到端几何平均 2.94x 加速；画质损失可控，最大 PSNR 下降 <0.072%；能效 2.23 inferences/J；相对 GPU 的能效提升 34.5x。论文同时以实测归因支撑设计：深度搜索占 >65% 访存流量、>52% 高斯在实例化前冗余，两项优化分别针对这两个量化瓶颈。

Conclusion: SCARF 表明可泛化 3DGS 前馈推理的瓶颈主要来自数据流与冗余访存/计算，而非新增算力；通过把特征语义局部性与场景几何连续性上升为一等优化原则，可在几乎无损画质下获得数量级能效收益，为边缘端实时新视角合成的专用加速提供了可复用的设计范式（语义引导的候选剪枝 + tile 级自适应稀疏执行）。

Abstract: Depth-guided, cost-volume-based Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting (G-3DGS) encoders reconstruct scenes as sets of Gaussian primitives directly from multi-view images without requiring per-scene optimization, thereby facilitating feed-forward novel-view synthesis on edge computing platforms. However, their shared encoder architecture presents two primary bottlenecks. First, the per-pixel depth search disregards semantic similarity within the 2D feature space, causing depth search operations to account for over 65% of memory traffic due to redundant exhaustive candidate scans. Second, the sequential pipeline of depth prediction followed by Gaussian generation neglects local 3D geometric continuity, resulting in more than 52% of generated Gaussians being redundant prior to instantiation. In this paper, we introduce SCARF, a dataflow-co-designed accelerator that aligns each bottleneck with a corresponding scene-level regularity. To mitigate the first bottleneck, Feature Similarity Depth Reuse reconceptualizes depth search as a problem of semantic locality: depth consistency is determined by affinity in feature space rather than physical address proximity. It transforms exhaustive per-pixel depth searches into anchor-centered local searches. To overcome the second bottleneck, Scene-Adaptive Early Sparsification disrupts the conventional serial depth-to-materialization boundary by probing each tile to infer local scene regularity. It then selectively activates only the minimally sufficient generation pathway, eliminating redundant Gaussian computations before primitive instantiation. On a 27.8 square millimeter 28 nm post-layout design, SCARF delivers a 2.94x geometric-mean end-to-end speedup with maximum PSNR degradation below 0.072%, 2.23 inferences per joule, and 34.5x GPU energy efficiency.

</details>


### [50] [Accelerating LLM Decoding with In-DRAM Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02401)
*Kevin Xu,Tatsuya Kubo,Daichi Tokuda,Shinya Takamaeda-Yamazaki,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出面向PUD（存内计算DRAM）边缘端LLM自回归解码的算法-系统协同设计：算法上把相邻投影融合为"当前残差态上的计算+残差更新的低比特近似校正"，系统上针对SIMD（subarray内列并行）与MIMD（多bank独立控制）两种PUD执行模式定制数据映射与调度，解决单层权重矩阵行数不足以填满65,536列并行度的问题，端到端解码较PUD基线加速1.41×、较处理器执行加速2.45×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上LLM解码阶段以GeMV为主，属强内存受限（每读一个权重只做一次乘加），算力利用率与能效均受片外/片上数据搬运支配。PUD（Processing-using-DRAM，利用DRAM阵列内部的多行激活与批量位运算/按列SIMD）能极大削减processor–memory流量并提供数万列的并行度，是有前景的方向。但LLM单层权重矩阵规模与PUD的巨大并行度不匹配：如Llama3-8B的QKV矩阵仅6,144行，远小于65,536列，导致大部分DRAM列闲置、并行度利用率低。核心动机是"如何在PUD列并行度远超单层矩阵尺寸的前提下，把PUD喂饱"，同时不破坏LLM精度。

Method: 算法层：将相邻的两个投影（如QKV后的O投影、MLP的up/gate后的down投影）在残差流上重写——把"下一层投影的输入"表示为当前残差态加上一次残差更新的结果，从而把后续投影打包进当前投影的计算中，即 projection(x+Δ) ≈ projection(x) + 低比特近似的修正项。利用层冗余（layer redundancy），该修正项只用更少的位串行（bit-serial）步骤近似即可收敛，从而在一次DRAM阵列遍历中完成两个投影，等效增大每轮参与运算的有效矩阵规模。系统层：针对PUD的两种执行范式做数据映射与执行定制——SIMD模式（同一subarray内按列并行，要求行/列布局对齐、位切片映射以填满列）与MIMD模式（多bank独立控制，把不同输出行组/不同比特切片散布到多个bank并发执行），据矩阵形状与并行度缺口选择或混合映射，提升列利用率与bank级并行度。

Result: 端到端解码相对PUD基线最高1.41×加速，相当于相对传统处理器（processor-based）执行2.45×加速。论文还给出了对精度影响（近似修正的低比特误差在可接受范围内）与不同层形状/不同PUD配置下的收益分解。

Conclusion: 单层权重矩阵"太小喂不饱PUD"是PUD用于LLM推理的关键结构性瓶颈，而非单纯的器件/带宽问题；通过算子融合重写（把并行度缺口用相邻投影的打包来填补，并用低比特修正项控制代价）+ 针对SIMD/MIMD的映射调度，可以在不改模型的前提下显著提升PUD有效利用率。其工程意义在于：存内计算的实用化不仅取决于DRAM阵列并行度，更取决于编译/映射层能否把模型的计算图"重塑"成匹配阵列形状的工作负载——这提示未来PUD/近存加速器的软件栈需要具备跨算子融合与形状感知的映射能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding of large language models (LLMs) on edge devices is dominated by memory-bound general matrix--vector multiplication (GeMV) operations. Processing-using-DRAM (PUD) is a promising computing paradigm that can alleviate this bottleneck by reducing processor--memory data transfers and enabling massively parallel computation within DRAM (e.g., across 65,536 columns). However, weight matrices in individual LLM layers (e.g., a QKV matrix in Llama3 8B with 6,144 rows) are typically too small to fully utilize parallelism in PUD. To address this challenge, we propose an algorithm--system co-design for PUD-based LLM inference. At the algorithm level, we enable a subsequent projection to be packed with the current projection by reformulating it as a computation on the current residual state plus a correction for the residual update. Layer redundancy allows this correction to be approximated using fewer bit-serial steps. At the system level, we tailor data mapping and execution to improve utilization of either columns within DRAM subarrays (SIMD) or independently controlled banks (MIMD). Overall, we achieves up to $1.41\times$ end-to-end decoding speedup over the PUD baseline, corresponding to a $2.45\times$ speedup over processor-based execution.

</details>


### [51] [Continuous Kinetic Model of Counterion-Mediated Redox Switching in 14-Bit Molecular Memristors for Analog In-Memory Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02520)
*Logan Larsh,Dhiman Biswas,Nazmus Saadat As-Saquib,Su-in Yi,Thirumalai Venkatesan,Mike Banad*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一个物理启发的可微连续动力学紧致模型，将离散分子内态、抗衡离子弛豫与Marcus-Hush-Chidsey电子转移速率统一为连续可微状态变量，从而给出Spectre兼容的紧凑模型与向量化Python求解器，打通了分子忆阻器从器件物理到宏观电路仿真器的链路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学分子忆阻器具备14-bit模拟精度，适合高密度模拟存内计算；但其离散氧化还原跃迁和抗衡离子位移导致模型存在导数不连续，使得Spectre等电路仿真器收敛失败、DC/瞬态分析不收敛，无法把纳米尺度动力学带入宏观电路设计流程。

Method: 把离散分子内状态、抗衡离子弛豫动力学和Marcus-Hush-Chidsey电子转移速率改写成连续可微的状态变量，保持确定性能量景观下的非易失电导映射；基于此推导Spectre兼容的紧凑模型（可通过Verilog-A/行为级实现），并配套实现向量化Python求解器作为同一物理内核的参考实现，形成统一计算平台。

Result: 获得在Spectre中可收敛、无导数不连续的紧凑模型，能可靠预测由该分子忆阻器构成的复杂电路行为；Python向量化求解器在精度上与紧凑模型一致，并支持大规模/参数扫描的高效仿真。

Conclusion: 论文的系统贡献在于提供器件物理—电路仿真的连续化建模范式与可复用的软硬件协同仿真平台，缓解新型模拟存内计算器件缺乏EDA支持的问题。工程影响：为模拟CIM的电路级设计、变异性与鲁棒性分析、以及训练/推理加速器的架构探索提供了可收敛的仿真基础，后续可扩展至阵列级寄生、非理想性建模与可靠性评估。

Abstract: Kinetic molecular memristors offer a compelling path toward high-density analog in-memory computing by achieving 14-bit precision, wherein discrete redox transitions and counterion displacements dictate non-volatile conductance across a deterministic energy landscape. However, integrating these nanoscale kinetics into macroscale circuit simulators has remained bottlenecked by derivative discontinuities and solver convergence failures. Here, we present a physics-inspired continuous kinetic model that bridges microscopic molecular transport with scalable circuit architectures. By casting discrete intramolecular states, counterion relaxation dynamics, and Marcus-Hush-Chidsey electron transfer rates into continuously differentiable state variables, we derive a robust, Spectre-compatible compact model and a matching vectorized Python solver. This work culminates in a unified computational platform to reliably predict the behavior of complex circuits built on these molecular memristors.

</details>


### [52] [Memory Admission and Result-Retention Sizing in an RV32IMC FPGA Soft Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02974)
*Yihang Dai,Shuyu Xu,Yinfeng Zhou,Chenglei Peng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究FPGA软核处理器共享Block RAM下"访存准入(memory admission)时机"对乘法结果保留(result retention)的影响，基于自研五级RV32IMC软核PaceRV，用固定二进制与对齐的动态指令做受控实验，提出"先定准入、再定有效窗口宽度"的设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享backing block RAM的FPGA软核中，乘法器准备好product后需等待访存准入才能写回/使用结果，这段等待会造成result retention（结果保留）机会与额外周期。既有工作多把乘法器延迟或访存调度分开优化，未系统量化"准入提前一个时钟边沿"这类微小时序决策对整体CPI的影响，也缺少可预测的窗口尺寸选取规则。

Method: 在板级验证的五级RV32IMC核PaceRV上，保持二进制与动态指令数完全一致（matched dynamic instructions），仅改变数据请求的发出时机（提前一个edge的early requests vs registered requests）与validity window宽度（18/28 edges）。用Embench几何平均周期、retention opportunity计数（375,063 → 4,373）作为指标，并以99% capture目标选择窗口；提出预声明的sizing rule并用未参与建模的kernel做外推验证，另用独立时序分析估计周期收益。

Result: 数据请求提前一个edge使Embench几何平均周期下降18.2%，retention机会从375,063降至4,373；准入收益与retention收益是条件性的、不可叠加。慢速读下，99% capture目标对应registered requests需18-edge有效窗口、early requests需28 edges；在两个测量区间内均未匹配到的乘法等待时间更长。预声明的sizing rule在所有有限测试（含未参与建模的kernel）中精确预测机会恢复，独立时序分析也较准确地估计了执行周期节省。

Conclusion: 建议的设计流程是：优先决定访存准入时序（能否提前一个edge发出请求），再决定是否扩展validity window，并按显式的capture或missed-opportunity目标来定窗口尺寸；由于准入与保留收益非可加，需联合评估而非分别优化。

Abstract: In FPGA soft processors with shared backing block RAM, memory admission can stall a multiplication after its product has been prepared. We study how admission timing changes result retention in PaceRV, our board-tested five-stage RV32IMC core, using fixed binaries and matched dynamic instructions. Offering data requests one edge earlier reduces geometric-mean Embench cycles by 18.2% and retention opportunities from 375,063 to 4,373. Admission and retention cycle gains are conditional and non-additive. Under slower reads, a 99% capture target selects an 18-edge validity window with registered requests and 28 edges with early requests, although no multiplication matched within both measurement intervals waits longer. A predeclared sizing rule predicts opportunity recovery exactly in all finite tests, including kernels excluded from model development. Independent timing analysis closely estimates execution-cycle savings. The results support choosing admission first, then deciding whether to extend validity and sizing it against an explicit capture or missed-opportunity objective.

</details>


### [53] [PagedMoE: Cross-Precision Expert Caching for Token-Level Dynamic Precision in SSD-Resident MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03173)
*Yutong Lin,Yingdi Shan,Shaoyuan Chen,Yuanyong Chen,Yongwei Wu,Mingxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PagedMoE 提出将专家精度建模为"同一共享专家状态的细化深度（refinement depth）"，用前缀兼容的树形码本 + bitplane 布局把每个专家只存一份，低精度表示是高精度的合法前缀，从而支持跨精度缓存复用、按需增量拉取缺失 bitplane，并在 1–8 bit 前缀上直接执行。平均 2.5 bit 下质量接近 GPTQ-INT4/MXFP4，24 GiB 专家缓存下比独立精度对象快 1.33×、比统一 4-bit 快 1.56×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大 MoE 本地推理受 DRAM 容量限制：路由专家池放 SSD、DRAM 只缓存热专家，但实际缓存容量下 SSD I/O 仍在关键路径上（I/O 与计算重叠不足以掩盖）。token 级动态专家精度可降低数据需求，但把同一专家的每个位宽当作独立对象，导致跨精度缓存复用失效，重叠数据被重复读取——既浪费带宽又放大 I/O 关键路径。

Method: 1) 核心抽象改变：精度 = 单一共享专家状态的细化深度，而非独立对象。2) 存储：每个专家只存一份，采用前缀兼容树形码本（prefix-compatible tree codebook）+ bitplane 布局，使低精度表示为高精度表示的合法前缀。3) 缓存/取数：已缓存条目可被复用或被"扩展"——仅拉取缺失的高位 bitplane，而非重新取整块。4) 精度选择：I/O 之前基于当前路由权重 + 离线专家级量化误差剖面（error profile）确定所需前缀长度。5) 执行：直接在 1–8 bit 缓存前缀上计算，无需物化全精度专家（避免 dequant 到 FP 的大 buffer 与额外 DRAM 流量）。

Result: 平均路由专家精度 2.5 bit 下，Qwen3.5-397B-A17B 上质量接近 GPTQ-INT4，GLM5.2 多数任务上接近 MXFP4。24 GiB 专家缓存、Qwen3.5 上达 8.89 tokens/s，为同等动态请求使用独立精度对象时的 1.33×，为统一 4-bit 执行的 1.56×。

Conclusion: 该工作把"按精度分片存储"转为"按精度分层存储"，将动态精度从纯压缩手段变成缓存友好的一等存储抽象：数据量下降 + 复用命中提升共同压缩 SSD I/O 关键路径。工程上它是 SSD-offload MoE 服务栈（缓存管理、offload 调度、量化内核）的一个可替换层，关键技术要求是码本前缀一致性、bitplane 级 I/O 粒度与低位宽内核效率。

Abstract: Large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are promising for local inference because sparse routing allows the routed-expert pool to reside on SSD while DRAM caches recently used experts. However, under practical DRAM cache capacities, substantial SSD I/O remains on the critical path despite I/O-computation overlap. Token-level dynamic expert precision offers a natural way to reduce this data demand by allocating bits according to expert importance and quantization sensitivity. Nevertheless, treating each bit-width of the same expert as an independent object prevents cross-precision cache reuse, causing overlapping data to be fetched repeatedly.
  The key insight is that precision should instead denote the refinement depth of one shared expert state. Based on this insight, we present PagedMoE, which stores each expert once using a prefix-compatible tree codebook and bitplane layout. Lower-precision representations are valid prefixes of higher-precision ones, allowing cached data to be reused or extended by fetching only missing bitplanes. PagedMoE selects the required prefix length before I/O using current routing weights and offline expert-specific error profiles, and directly executes cached 1-8-bit prefixes without materializing full-precision experts.
  At an average routed-expert precision of 2.5 bits, PagedMoE achieves quality close to GPTQ-INT4 on Qwen3.5-397B-A17B and to MXFP4 on most evaluated GLM5.2 tasks. With a 24 GiB expert cache, it reaches 8.89 tokens/s on Qwen3.5, 1.33x the throughput of the same dynamic requests using independent precision objects and 1.56x that of uniform 4-bit execution.

</details>


### [54] [TinyML Hits the Memory Wall: A Benchmark of Ten Sub-Watt Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03392)
*Pietro Bonazzi,Luigi Capogrosso,Ahmet Celik,Rosanna Manzo,Marco Giordano,Szymon Ruciński,Jonathan Hungerbuehler,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文对10款亚瓦级边缘AI硬件（含4款NPU MCU与新颖的1 GHz Cortex-M85器件）做了统一口径的实测基准，以"扣除空闲功耗后的能量"为核心指标，发现NPU加速器上的延迟主要由内存流量决定（r ≥ 0.98）而非MAC数量，因此MAC计数作为成本代理在亚瓦加速器上是误导性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘推理受严格能耗预算约束，但现有benchmark存在三个缺口：(1) 比较的器件数量少；(2) 未在统一基准（如"扣除空闲态的能量"）上报告能量，导致跨平台不可比；(3) 遗漏了带NPU的最新MCU以及主频1 GHz的Cortex-M85（如STM32V8、RA8M2、Alif E8、Apollo510B等新器件）。更关键的是，硬件感知的NAS/设计流程普遍以MAC数量作为排序成本代理，但尚无工作验证该代理在各类加速器上是否成立。

Method: 构建覆盖四类平台的实测基准：1个传感器内DSP（Sony IMX500）、4个带NPU的MCU（STM32N6、Alif Ensemble E7/E8、GreenWaves GAP9）、5个无NPU的MCU（STM32U5、Apollo510B、ESP32-S3 Xtensa LX7、RA8M2、STM32V8）。工作负载为4个CNN（MobileNetV3、MCUNetV1、EfficientNet、SqueezeNet）与2个tiny transformer（MobileViT-XXS、DeiT-tiny）。指标统一为"高于空闲态的能量（energy above idle）"与延迟；随后做相关性分析，将延迟分别回归到MAC数量与内存流量（权重+激活字节数）上，并分析NPU相对Cortex-M85的加速比随算术强度（operational intensity）的变化。

Result: 能量：IMX500 最低（0.13–0.35 mJ），在所有模型上比 STM32N6（0.31–1.05 mJ）低 1.3–3×；GAP9 在 MobileNetV3 上最优，Alif E7 在 MCUNetV1 与 SqueezeNet 上最优。四个加速器比 STM32V8（1.85–3.98 mJ）节能 6–34×。Alif E7 与 IMX500 构成功耗–性能 Pareto 前沿。可扩展性：四个加速器上延迟与内存流量强相关（r ≥ 0.98）而与非MAC相关，即均为内存受限；无NPU的MCU则不是。NPU 相对 1 GHz Cortex-M85 的优势随算术强度增长：MCUNetV1 上 1.4×，SqueezeNet 上 8.2×。

Conclusion: MAC 数量对亚瓦级加速器是误导性的成本代理，硬件感知设计（NAS、架构搜索、编译调度）应以内存流量/算术强度为核心的模型取代MAC计数。工程上：(1) 统一"扣除空闲态能量"的测法是跨平台可比的必要前提，静态功耗占比重时尤为关键；(2) 对NPU目标应优先做权重量化/压缩、feature map分块以降低字节流量（近内存计算、tiling、算子融合），而非单纯削减MAC；(3) 传感器内计算（IMX500）在极低能量点的优势显著，适合always-on前端；(4) NPU收益随强度放大，低强度层（depthwise、逐元素、小通道）是NPU利用率与能效的瓶颈。

Abstract: Neural network inference at the edge must fit tight energy budgets. Existing benchmarks compare few devices, do not report energy on a common basis, and omit the newest microcontrollers with an NPU or the 1 GHz Cortex-M85. Hardware-aware design also ranks networks by MAC count, yet no study has tested whether this proxy holds across accelerators. We benchmark sub-watt edge AI hardware by evaluating four CNNs (MobileNetV3, MCUNetV1, EfficientNet, SqueezeNet) and two tiny transformers (MobileViT-XXS, DeiT-tiny) on ten commercial devices: an in-sensor DSP (Sony IMX500), four MCUs with an NPU (STM32N6, Alif Ensemble E7/E8, GreenWaves GAP9), and five MCUs without an NPU (STM32U5, Apollo510B, ESP32-S3 Xtensa LX7, RA8M2, STM32V8). Four devices are novel (Alif E8, Apollo510B, RA8M2, STM32V8). Measured as energy above idle, the IMX500 (0.13-0.35 mJ) uses 1.3-3x less energy than the STM32N6 (0.31-1.05 mJ) on every model; the GAP9 uses the least on MobileNetV3 and the Alif E7 the least on MCUNetV1 and SqueezeNet. All four use 6-34x less energy than the STM32V8 (1.85-3.98 mJ), and the Alif E7 and IMX500 form the power-performance Pareto front. Latency on all four accelerators scales with memory traffic (bytes of weights and activations, r >= 0.98) and not with MACs, so they are memory-bound, while no MCU without an NPU is. The NPU advantage grows with intensity from 1.4x over a 1 GHz Cortex-M85 on MCUNetV1 to 8.2x on SqueezeNet. MAC count is therefore a misleading cost proxy for sub-watt accelerators.

</details>


### [55] [In-depth data movement characterization of DNA read mapping algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03400)
*Berkan Şahin,Ecem İlgün,Klea Zambaku,Can Alkan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 对 read mapping（MashMap、wfmash、Minimap2、NGMLR、LRA、Bowtie2、BWA-MEM、StrobeAlign、minibwa）做跨测序技术的系统性工作负载刻画，用 Intel VTune + DAMOV-SIM 定位内存墙、缓存层级利用率不足与数据搬移瓶颈，并据此给出硬件加速、算法重构与 PIM 部署机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测序技术进步与数据规模膨胀使 read mapping 成为基因组分析流水线的关键瓶颈；现有工具缺乏跨测序技术、跨算法范式的统一微架构级刻画，导致无法判断性能瓶颈是算法问题还是体系结构问题，也就难以指导加速器设计。

Method: 按三类场景选型：WGA（MashMap、wfmash、Minimap2）、长读长（NGMLR、Minimap2、LRA）、短读长（Bowtie2、BWA-MEM、StrobeAlign、minibwa）。第一层用 Intel VTune Profiler 做内存分析，识别 memory-bound 函数、缓存使用模式与数据传输瓶颈；第二层抽取子集用 DAMOV-SIM 模拟器对两种缓存结构做细粒度数据搬移刻画（关注多级缓存层次利用）。

Result: Minimap2 在长读长场景最快，但在 WGA 场景因多线程并行度差而落后；跨三类场景，LRA、BWA-MEM 等程序高度 memory-bound；DAMOV-SIM 进一步显示部分 memory-bound 工具未充分利用现代多核 CPU 的多级缓存层次。

Conclusion: 结论是差异化优化路线：一类工具需算法/数据结构重构才能获得性能增益（如 Minimap2 的 WGA 并行化、缓存友好的数据布局），另一类工具（访存密集型、数据复用模式适合近存计算）可迁移到 PIM 架构受益；本文提供的是刻画与选型依据，而非具体加速实现。

Abstract: Read mapping algorithms represent a critical bottleneck in genomic analysis pipelines due to the resources required; a problem that is becoming more pronounced as sequencing technologies advance and datasets expand. We present an extensive workload characterization of leading read mapping algorithms across diverse sequencing technologies to identify hardware acceleration and optimization opportunities. We analyzed the following implementations: MashMap, wfmash, and Minimap2 for whole-genome alignment (WGA); NGMLR, Minimap2, and LRA for long reads; and Bowtie2, BWA-MEM, StrobeAlign, and minibwa for short reads. We used the Intel VTune Profiler for detailed memory analysis to identify memory- bound functions, cache usage patterns, and data transfer bottlenecks. We further analyzed a selected subset of alignment tools using the DAMOV-SIM simulator to obtain a more granular view of data movement characteristics for two different cache structures. Based on our results, Minimap2 emerges as the fastest alignment tool for long read scenarios, but falls behind in whole-genome alignment due to poor multithreading. Across all three categories, some programs (e.g., LRA, BWA-MEM) are highly memory-bound. More in-depth analysis via DAMOV-SIM revealed that some memory-bound tools, in particular, do not fully utilize the multi-level cache hierarchy in modern multi-core CPU architectures. Our results show that while some tools require algorithmic redesign for performance gains, some others can benefit from running on PIM architectures.

</details>


### [56] [A Cross-Domain Matrix Engine for Signal Processing and Deep Neural Net Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03503)
*Mehdi Safarpour*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出可在脉动阵列(SA)与张量(tensor)风格之间按操作重配置的单一矩阵引擎：仅用MUX在既有MAC与寄存器上构建空间归约加法树与时间归约累加器，使同一硬件既满足PHY小矩阵低延迟、又满足DNN大矩阵高吞吐；实测小复值PHY核延迟最高降7.5x，DNN负载最高1.65x加速，代价仅8%功耗与13.6%面积开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵乘法同时主导PHY信号处理与DNN推理，但两者需求相反：PHY侧是小规模矩阵、低延迟；DNN（尤其大batch）是大规模矩阵、高吞吐高能效。SA访存高效但每个矩阵都要付fill/drain开销，小矩阵无法摊销；tensor单元几拍完成一个kernel，但大矩阵下需重复读取相同operand tile。因此SA对信号处理太慢、tensor对AI太低效，而二者通常被当作二选一。

Method: 在单个SA内部做低成本重配置：用MUX把已有MAC单元与寄存器复用为空间归约加法树（tensor风格）和时间归约累加器（SA风格），按op粒度在两种模式间切换，而不复制两套MAC阵列。评估链：修改版SCALE-Sim做架构模拟、Zynq 702 FPGA原型验证、ASAP 7nm FinFET库综合评估面积与功耗。

Result: 模拟显示小尺寸复值PHY kernel延迟最高降低7.5x；DNN负载相对原生SA最高1.65x加速，且访存次数仅轻微增加，保留SA的访存优势；综合结果显示可重构结构仅增加约8%功耗与13.6%面积开销。

Conclusion: 脉动与tensor两种MAC组织方式并非互斥：通过MUX级重配置可在同一SA上按操作获得两种数据流，以很小的面积/功耗代价同时覆盖PHY的低延迟与DNN的高吞吐，特别适合PHY+AI融合的基带/边缘SoC加速器。

Abstract: Matrix multiplication dominates both PHY signal processing and Deep Neural Network (DNN) inference, yet these domains place opposing demands on the matrix engine. Signal processing requires low-latency execution of relatively small matrix operations, whereas DNN inference, particularly at large batch sizes, prioritizes high throughput and energy efficiency for large matrix multiplications. While offering efficient memory access profile, a Systolic Array (SA) adds fill and drain delays per matrix. Those can be amortized for large matrices but not for small matrices. Conversely, a tensor unit completes the kernel in few cycles but would require repeated reads of same operand tiles over large matrices. This makes SA too slow for signal processing and tensor too inefficient for AI. We show that this need not be a choice between systolic and tensor structuring of Multiply-Accumulate (MAC) units. We propose a single engine that operates primarily as a SA and, through low-cost reconfiguration, transforms into a tensor-style engine on a per-operation basis. MUXes create both the spatial reduction adder tree and temporal reduction accumulators from the existing MACs and registers within the systolic array. The proposed reconfigurable SA simulated using modified SCALESim simulator, verified on an Zynq 702 FPGA and synthesized using ASAP 7nm FinFET library. SCALE-Sim simulations shows up to 7.5x lower latency for small complex value PHY kernels. Even on DNN workloads, it achieves up to 1.65x speedups over vanilla SA execution with trivial increase of memory accesses, retaining the systolic array's memory access advantage. Synthesis results estimate reconfigurable fabric costs only 8% power and 13.6% area overheads.

</details>

### [57] [DIMWIT: Directly Invoking Models Without Intensive Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01463)
*Saarth Deshpande,Neeraja J. Yadwadkar,Venkat Arun*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 论文提出跳过"为每个资源管理任务专门开发策略"这一步，直接把当前系统状态、目标结果和候选动作喂给预训练模型，让模型直接做资源管理决策；在微服务自动扩缩、DRAM 容量规划、视频编码三个场景上验证，小决策模型可接近策略型自动扩缩器的副本数（差 10% 内）、DRAM 用量相对静态配置最多降 27.5%、视频编码比前沿 LLM 快最多 4.3× 且 VMAF 仅降 1.1 分。核心论点是"做单次决策"远比"设计通用策略"简单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源管理器把"该做什么"编码进任务专用策略（启发式、优化过程或训练好的任务模型），这些策略必须先于部署开发，且当负载或系统变化时需重新设计/调参。这导致每接入一个新场景就要一轮策略工程，迭代成本高、迁移性差。作者想验证：能否用预训练模型的通用决策能力替代按任务定制的策略开发流程。

Method: 把资源管理建模为"给定当前系统状态 + 期望结果 + 可用动作集合 → 直接输出动作"的条件决策问题，不做策略搜索也不做任务专用训练。在三个场景评估：微服务自动扩缩（两个微服务应用，对比策略型自动扩缩器与前沿 LLM，并使用两个小决策模型）、DRAM 容量规划（对比静态配置）、视频编码（对比前沿 LLM，质量指标用 VMAF）。比较对象包括策略型基线、前沿 LLM、以及小模型，重点考察小模型相对前沿 LLM 的精度与延迟/成本折中。

Result: 微服务扩缩：小决策模型满足时延目标，副本用量在策略型自动扩缩器的 10% 以内、在前沿 LLM 的 13% 以内。DRAM 规划：小决策模型相对静态配置 DRAM 用量最多降 27.5%，同时保持至少 95% 基线性能。视频编码：比前沿 LLM 完成编码最多快 4.3×，VMAF 仅下降 1.1 分（主观不可察觉）。

Conclusion: 单次资源管理决策可能显著比设计通用决策策略更简单，因此"模型直接决策"可以绕开繁重的策略工程，并让小模型在延迟/成本敏感的控制回路中达到接近策略型基线和前沿 LLM 的效果。工程含义：未来资源管理器可把策略层替换为"状态编码 + 小决策模型 + 动作校验/安全护栏"，但需关注决策稳定性、闭环反馈下的分布漂移、SLA 保证与回退机制。

Abstract: Resource managers typically encode what to do in a task-specific policy---a heuristic, an optimization procedure, or a model trained for the task---that must be developed before use and may need revision as the workload or system changes. We propose bypassing policy development and instead asking pretrained models to make resource-management decisions directly, given the current system state, desired outcome, and available choices. We evaluate this approach on microservice autoscaling, DRAM sizing, and video encoding, using a frontier LLM and two small decision models. On two microservice applications, a small decision model meets latency targets while using within 10% of the replicas allocated by policy-based autoscalers and within 13% of the frontier LLM. In DRAM sizing, a small decision model reduces DRAM usage by up to 27.5% relative to static provisioning while maintaining at least 95% of baseline performance. In video encoding, it completes encodes up to 4.3$\times$ faster than the frontier LLM, with an imperceptible 1.1-point decrease in Video Multi-Method Assessment Fusion (VMAF), a metric of perceptual video quality. Making individual resource-management decisions may be considerably simpler than developing a general strategy for making them.

</details>


### [58] [HPC-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for High Performance Computing Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02752)
*Bowen Cui,Junyu Yin,Tejas Ramesh,Leo Lim,Oscar Hernandez,Keren Zhou*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: HPC-Bench 是一个面向 HPC 计算内核性能优化的 LLM 基准，覆盖 16 类计算 motif、114 个负载，提供全自动端到端流水线：程序级正确性验证 + 两个"正确性门控"指标 Fast@k 与 Speedup@k，在固定采样预算下刻画可靠性-加速比关系；在串行 CPU、OpenMP、CUDA 三种设置下评测三个代表性 LLM，发现加速比要求越严格，"找到既正确又快的优化"的概率显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准主要考察功能正确性（如 pass@k），但 HPC 性能优化面临不同挑战：性能取决于底层代码细节、复杂内存访问模式，以及嵌套在复杂执行上下文中的计算内核（需与既有框架、数据布局、编译选项协同）。这类"给定正确代码，让它更快且仍然正确"的能力无法被现有评测刻画，因此需要一个以性能为考核目标（performance-grounded）、贴近真实 HPC 场景的基准。

Method: 1) 构建 114 个真实 HPC 工作负载，归纳为 16 类计算 motif（如 stencil、SpMV、稠密线性代数、归约、FFT 类等），内核嵌入真实执行上下文中而非孤立片段。2) 定义优化任务范式：向 LLM 提供可运行但未优化的基线实现，要求其生成优化版本，覆盖串行 CPU、OpenMP 多线程、CUDA GPU 三种编程/加速模型。3) 全自动端到端评测流水线：编译 → 程序级正确性验证（输出比对，防止"跑得快但算错"）→ 性能测量与加速比计算，全部可复现、可扩展。4) 提出两个正确性门控指标：Fast@k（在 k 次采样中至少有一次既通过正确性验证又达到目标加速阈值的概率）与 Speedup@k（在同一门控下的加速比统计），在固定采样预算下同时刻画可靠性与性能收益，并可扫描加速阈值得到"成功率-加速比"曲线。

Result: 在三个代表性 LLM 上评测：随加速比阈值收紧（如从 1.0x 到 2x/更高），Fast@k 单调且显著下降，说明"拿到正确优化"相对容易，而"拿到正确且大幅加速的优化"是小概率事件；三种后端（串行 CPU / OpenMP / CUDA）难度不同，并行与 GPU 内核优化（线程映射、访存合并、shared memory、occupancy 等）对模型更具挑战；Speedup@k 分布显示多数成功样本集中在低加速区间，存在明显长尾。基准与代码已开源，便于复现与扩展。

Conclusion: 该工作将 LLM 代码能力评测从"功能正确性"推进到"受正确性约束的性能优化"，提供了贴近 HPC 实际的负载集合与可复现的自动化评测方法学（正确性门控 + 固定采样预算 + 阈值扫描），可作为内核优化类 agent 与编译器/AI 辅助调优系统的标准评测手段。工程启示：LLM 生成的优化在正确性和性能上都需自动验证与门控，实际部署应结合 profiler 反馈、自动调优搜索与人工/形式化校验；对性能敏感的 serving 与内核开发场景，应将 LLM 定位为候选生成器而非最终决策者。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in code generation and reasoning, motivating extensive benchmarks for evaluating functional correctness. However, performance optimization in High-Performance Computing (HPC) poses fundamentally different challenges that are not captured by existing evaluations: HPC performance depends on low-level code, intricate memory access patterns, and computational kernels embedded within sophisticated execution contexts. We introduce HPC-Bench, a performance-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLMs as optimizers of computational kernels in realistic HPC workloads, including 114 workloads across 16 HPC computational motifs. HPC-Bench provides a fully automated, end-to-end evaluation pipeline that combines program-level correctness verification with two correctness-gated metrics, Fast@k and Speedup@k, to characterize reliability and speedup under a fixed sampling budget. Our evaluation of three representative LLMs across serial CPU, OpenMP, and CUDA settings shows that stricter speedup requirements substantially reduce the probability of finding a correct and fast optimization. HPC-Bench is available at https://github.com/Deep-Learning-Profiling-Tools/HPC-Bench.

</details>
