### [59] [An Agentic OS Kernel for Flexible and Enhanced Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00002)
*Haocheng Wang,Xinyue Sun,Hao Yi,Dong Du,Yubin Xia,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 论文提出 agentic kernel（Linux 实现名为 IntentOS）：把单体 syscall 拆解为细粒度、可组合的内核原生 micro-syscall，并引入 isyscall（intent syscall）让应用提交高层意图，由 LLM 将其编译为 micro-syscall 组合计划并在运行时持续优化；通过缓存/复用已验证计划把 LLM 推理移出关键路径，且安全性只依赖 syscall 级既有安全机制、与 LLM 正确性解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 OS 内核接口通用但僵化：syscall 因兼容性、性能与安全约束演进缓慢，应用被迫承受语义错配与遗留 API 开销；eBPF 等内核机制定制方案虽提升灵活性，但受严格安全/性能限制、需要高门槛专家经验，且面向"内核机制"而非"内核抽象"。目标是让内核抽象本身具备可编程性与动态适配能力。

Method: 1) 将传统 syscall 功能分解为细粒度、可组合的 kernel-native micro-syscall 集合；2) 新增入口 isyscall，接收应用的高层 intent 描述；3) 由内核侧 LLM 将 intent 解析/编译为 micro-syscall 组合计划，并在运行时持续优化该计划（如剪除冗余、合并路径）；4) 计划缓存与复用：已验证的 plan 直接绕过 LLM，使 LLM 推理不落在关键路径上；5) 安全边界设计：micro-syscall 沿用 syscall 级安全机制（权限检查、审计等），保证任意组合与编排下的 OS 安全性，安全性与 LLM 输出正确性解耦。

Result: 在 Linux 上实现 IntentOS，跨 4 个内核版本、3 类应用域（每域约 10 个 intent）评测：相比基线 Linux，后端进程创建延迟最多降低 17.55%；多文件操作性能提升 6.97%；后端任务触发内存操作时，对前端 Nginx 服务端的性能干扰降低 75.52%。

Conclusion: 该工作将"LLM 作为内核抽象层的编排器"与"syscall 级安全保障"解耦组合，论证了内核 ABI 可以变成可组合、可演化的目标，同时不牺牲安全性；其工程价值在于用 plan 缓存把 LLM 的非确定性与尾部延迟隔离在慢路径，使语义级适配（如跨进程/文件/内存的批量语义合并）成为可能。局限与待验证点：LLM 生成与验证链路的可信度、plan 缓存的命中率与失效策略、micro-syscall 组合的验证开销与攻击面扩大、跨版本可移植性及多租户/隔离场景下的适用性，以及"由内核调用 LLM"带来的可观测性、调试与资源计量问题仍需深入评估。

Abstract: Modern OS kernels expose general-purpose but rigid interfaces (e.g., syscalls) that are slow to evolve due to compatibility, performance, and security constraints, leaving apps to pay for mismatched semantics and legacy APIs. Existing kernel mechanism customization approaches (e.g., eBPF) improve flexibility but still face strict safety and performance restrictions and thus require substantial expertise. What's more, they targets customized kernel mechanisms rather than kernel abstractions.
  This paper presents agentic kernel, a new architecture that decomposes monolithic syscall functions into fine-grained, composable kernel-native micro-syscalls, and then enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to orchestrate them dynamically. Agentic kernel introduces a new entry, isyscall (intent syscall), which lets apps submit high-level intents describing their requirements. Then the kernel leverages LLMs to resolve each intent into composition plan of micro-syscalls to satisfy the user's goal. LLMs can further optimize the plan continuously at kernel runtime. To eliminate LLM inference costs on the critical path, agentic kernel caches and reuses verified plans to bypass LLM entirely. Crucially, agentic kernel's safety guarantees are independent of LLM correctness: micro-syscalls follows syscall-level security mechanisms, ensuring OS safety under arbitrary combinations and orchestration.
  We implement agentic kernel in Linux, called IntentOS, and evaluate it across four kernel versions and three application domains (about ten intents each). IntentOS outperforms baseline Linux in real-world applications, achieving up to 17.55% latency reduction for backend processes creation, 6.97% performance gain for multiple files operations, and 75.52% lower performance impact for frontend Nginx server during memory operations triggered by backend tasks.

</details>


### [60] [TenonAgent: Virtualizing Execution Residency for Memory-Oversubscribed LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00051)
*Yifan Zhang,Xinkui Zhao,Ruixin Huang,Lezhuo Xuan,Dianyu Wang,Siying Sun,Xiaoyi Li,Hengxuan Lou,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: TenonAgent 是一个面向 LLM Agent 长周期多阶段工作流的运行时系统，通过将 agent 逻辑生命周期与物理沙箱执行环境解耦（sandbox residency 虚拟化），以“动作边界 + 工具执行期间”的双时间尺度控制面（预测性准入控制 + 基于 sched_ext 的内核节流门）在高度内存超卖下抑制 OOM 与重试，实现在 8.6x 内存超卖比下 100% 任务成功率，相对无管理执行最高 5.5x 加速，并优于现有 agent 级与 cgroup 级调度器 5–37x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 以长生命周期、多阶段工作流方式执行，不同阶段（推理、工具调用、I/O 等待）资源需求差异巨大，且存在明显空闲间隙。按生命周期峰值预留（provision for peak）导致内存利用率极低、并发受限；而激进超卖又会引发内存争用、OOM 与代价高昂的整条工作流重试。现有 agent 级调度器与 cgroup 级内存控制（如 memory.max + 回收/OOM killer）粒度不匹配：cgroup 只能做粗粒度压制，缺乏对 agent 语义（动作边界）与可安全中断点的感知，也无法在工具执行期间对并发内存增长做前瞻性反馈控制。核心矛盾是主机内存受限下“并发度 vs 失败率”的权衡。

Method: 1) Sandbox residency 虚拟化：把 agent 的逻辑身份/状态与 Docker 容器或 Firecracker microVM 的物理驻留解耦，支持沙箱的 materialize/dematerialize（换出与重建），使物理资源占用可随全局内存压力动态伸缩。2) 双时间尺度控制面：慢尺度在“动作边界”（agent 步骤间，天然的可中断/可重建点）做预测性准入控制，结合 fail-closed、restart-safe 的契约——若预测到内存不足则拒绝或延迟启动，并保证被驱逐的沙箱可从检查点安全重启，语义上不产生错误结果；快尺度在工具执行期间通过 sched_ext 的 eBPF 调度器实现内核态“门控”，对沙箱执行权限进行节流（限制可运行并发度），抑制内存的突发并发增长，并把门控反馈回上层用于后续执行机会的分配。3) 工程假设：状态外置/检查点化使 dematerialize 可恢复；sched_ext 允许在不改内核主线的前提下实现可插拔的调度策略并与其用户态控制环协同。

Result: 在多样化 agentic 工作负载下：突发场景达到 8.6x 内存超卖比时任务成功率 100%，OOM 失败显著下降；相对无管理执行最高 5.5x 加速；相对现有 agent 级与 cgroup 级调度器加速 5x–37x；在 Docker 与 Firecracker microVM 两种后端上收益一致，说明机制对容器/微虚机抽象层不敏感。

Conclusion: 该工作把 agent serving 的资源管理问题重新表述为“生命周期与物理驻留解耦 + 双时间尺度闭环控制”，其系统贡献在于将应用语义（动作边界、可重启契约）与内核可编程调度（sched_ext）结合，形成可落地的内存超卖控制面，而非单纯调参。工程影响：为 agent 平台提供高密度共置方案，直接降低每 agent 的内存占用与失败重试成本；但也带来新开销与风险需评估——dematerialize/materialize 的延迟与状态持久化带宽、重启的幂等性要求、可预测准入模型对工作负载漂移的鲁棒性、以及 sched_ext 门控在 CPU 争用与尾延迟上的副作用。抽象未给出公平性、隔离强度、检查点/恢复开销与多租户 SLO 的数据，实际部署需关注门控参数与准入阈值在高负载下的稳定性。

Abstract: LLM agents execute as long-lived, multi-stage workflows with substantial resource-demand variation across phases. Provisioning each agent or sandbox for its lifetime peak leads to low resource utilization, particularly for memory, whereas aggressive oversubscription risks memory contention, out-of-memory failures, and costly workflow retries. This tension limits the concurrency and efficiency of agent serving under constrained host memory.
  We present TenonAgent, a runtime system that virtualizes sandbox residency by decoupling the logical agent's lifetime from its physical execution environment. To manage memory oversubscription, TenonAgent employs a dual-timescale control plane. At action boundaries, it combines predictive admission control with a fail-closed, restart-safe contract, dynamically materializing or dematerializing sandboxes based on global memory pressure. During active tool execution, a sched_ext-based kernel gate paces sandbox execution permissions to reduce unexpected concurrent memory growth. The gate provides feedback control over future execution opportunities.
  Evaluations across diverse agentic workloads show that, under the tested workloads and parameter settings, TenonAgent substantially reduces observed OOM failures and achieves a 100% task success rate in bursts reaching an 8.6x memory oversubscription ratio. Overall, it delivers up to a 5.5x speedup over unmanaged execution, outperforms existing agent-level and cgroup-based schedulers by 5x to 37x, and provides consistent benefits across both Docker and Firecracker microVM backends.

</details>


### [61] [Memorix: Managing Memory Selections for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00060)
*Jing Zhang,Xiaguannan Song,Dong Du,Erhu Feng,Yubin Xia*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Memorix 提出"预计算式 agent 记忆"：把 agent 该读什么上下文的选择过程从"用时临时拼装"改为"按项目一次性离线计算、跨任务复用"，通过不可变版本化 Store、可复现 View（Derivation/Environment 分离）、以及 Quest/Profile 刷新机制实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 agent 记忆系统在推理时才临时把选中的文件拼进 prompt：一是每个任务都重复做上下文组装，摊销成本高；二是临时选择可能漏掉关键材料；三是组装过程不可追踪、不可重放，失败难调试；四是 agent 可能读到与项目版本不匹配的文档（版本错配）。核心观察是：agent 该读的内容主要由项目本身决定，因此选择可以提前算好并复用。

Method: 三个机制：(1) 不可变、带版本的 Store：按内容身份固定每个源文件，能以低成本复现历史快照/布局，为可重放提供基础；(2) View：把"选择"（Derivations）与"组装"（Environment）解耦，使结果可复现、可共享，Environment 是预计算产物；(3) Quests 与 Profiles：让已交付的 view 在源演化时仍可刷新，并在项目内保持可用。实现后在 SWE-ContextBench 与 LibEvolutionEval 上与主流 agent 记忆系统对比。

Result: 预计算 Environment 使解决率最高提升 1.5 倍；在所有版本条件化任务上均交付正确的库版本；摊销成本最高降低 100 倍，构建时间最高降低 38 倍。

Conclusion: 把上下文选择从"在线拼装"转为"离线预计算 + 版本化复用"，能在提升正确性与可复现性的同时大幅降低摊销开销，是 agent 记忆/上下文工程的一个有前景的系统方向；但预计算的有效性依赖项目状态变化频率，需配合刷新机制（Quest/Profile）控制陈旧性。

Abstract: AI agents now handle a growing share of tasks across sessions within a persistent project. Their behavior depends not only on the model, but heavily on the persistent state they read from the environment. Existing systems assemble this memory at use time, splicing selected files into the prompt as a one-off payload. As a result, per-task assembly is costly and can still miss critical material, failures cannot be traced or replayed, and agents can read documentation written for the wrong version. Our key insight is that what an agent should read is mostly determined by its project, so the selection can be computed once, ahead of time, and reused across tasks.
  We propose Memorix, an agent memory system that realizes this insight. Memorix achieves this via three key mechanisms: (1) an immutable and versioned Store pins every source by identity and reproduces historical layouts cheaply, (2) a View that splits selection (Derivations) from composition (Environment), making the result reproducible and shareable, and (3) Quests and Profiles keep a delivered view refreshable and available inside the project as sources evolve. We implement Memorix and evaluate it on SWE-ContextBench and LibEvolutionEval against mainstream agent memory systems. Precomputed Environments raise resolved rate by up to 1.5X and deliver the correct library version on every version-conditioned task, while cutting amortized cost by up to 100X and build time by up to 38X.

</details>


### [62] [Beyond Source Compatibility: WebAssembly as a Stable ABI for ARINC 653 Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00891)
*Wanja Zaeske,Patrick Siegl,Florian Hartung,Fabian Bauriedl,Nicolaus Bär,Umut Durak*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 论文针对ARINC 653 API在数据布局、位宽等实现细节上仍依赖具体OS的问题，精化头文件消除歧义，并基于WebAssembly定义OS与处理器架构无关的二进制级ABI，同时用VM无关的shim库在两种独立Wasm运行时、多种处理器架构上运行ARINC 653分区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARINC 653为IMA提供的仅是源码级API，内存布局、部分整型位宽等实现相关，跨OS移植需重编译甚至改源码，缺乏对象码级的可移植契约。

Method: 三步：一是精化ARINC 653头文件，消除残留语义歧义；二是以Wasm为编译目标，定义面向对象码的OS/架构无关ABI，显式处理数据布局、端序和函数指针表示的host/guest差异；三是实现与Wasm VM无关的shim库，将ARINC 653分区编译为Wasm字节码后加载执行。

Result: 在两个独立的Wasm运行时和多种处理器架构上成功执行遵循该ABI的ARINC 653分区，验证了ABI的可移植性与运行时无关性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种以Wasm作为可移植执行与ABI载体的IMA软件分发路径，将分区可移植性从源码级推进到二进制级。

Abstract: Thirty years ago, ARINC 653 was introduced to provide a vendor-independent platform for software applications in Integrated Modular Avionics (IMA). A platform is characterized by a well-defined interface abstracting over implementation details; in the case of ARINC 653 this is an Application Programming Interface (API), defined on the source-code level. However, several aspects of this API - such as the memory layout and partially also the integer bit width of data types - remain implementation dependent, so portability across Operating Systems (OSs) requires recompilation, and often also source code adaptation. Our contribution is three-fold; first, we refine the ARINC 653 header file, eliminating remaining ambiguities. Second, building on WebAssembly (Wasm), we define an OS- and processor-architecture-agnostic Application Binary Interface (ABI) at the object-code level, resolving differences in data layout, endianness, and function-pointer representation between host and guest environments. Third, we demonstrate a Wasm-VM-agnostic shim-library that executes ARINC 653 partitions, compiled to Wasm bytecode and following our ABI, on multiple processor architectures using two independent Wasm runtimes.

</details>


### [63] [RTP-LLM: Building an Evolvable and Reliable LLM Inference Engine at Alibaba Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00967)
*Jiarui Guo,Zhi Zhang,Boyu Tan,Zongwei Lv,Haobo Sun,Na He,Ziqing Wang,Xinfei Shi,Wei Zhang,Xi Yang,Haosheng Hu,Wending Bao,Zetao Hu,Dong Cao,Yong Li,Lining Wang,Chengjun Yang,Tianpeng Wang,Huanbing Lu,Yidan Wang,Hengyi Bao,Yaxin Yu,Hanyun Guo,Huidong Zhang,Haiyu Zhou,Zefeng Liu,Weixiang Huang,Ziyi Wang,Chenyi Song,Ruixuan Zhang,Zhengkai Ao,Haocheng Luo,Zhichen Xue,Jialin Zhang,Chi Zhang,Lingjun Zhu,Silu Zhou,Zongyuan Wu,Yinghao Yu,Yi Zheng,Miao Sun,Yuxing Yuan,Juncheng Yin,Guoding Li,Nan Wang,Xiyu Wang,Lin Yang,Zechao Zhang,Jianning Zhang,Bo Cai,Xiaolin Wang,Tao Lan,Tong Yang,Kan Liu,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: RTP-LLM 是阿里巴巴开源并大规模部署的生产级推理引擎，通过将"模型特定执行"与"共享的请求处理、状态管理、资源协调"解耦，使新模型接入从一次性集成变为可复用的常规任务；已支撑约 100 个模型、每日处理数万亿级 token，并给出在异构生产环境中兼顾快速演进与可靠性的架构与运维经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 与推理技术高速迭代，模型接入在生产中成为反复发生的工作而非一次集成；共享推理基础设施既要快速跟进新模型与新服务能力，又要在异构生产环境中保持高可靠，二者存在张力。论文动机是把这一"演进性 vs 可靠性"矛盾作为首要设计约束，而非仅优化单模型吞吐。

Method: 架构上将模型特定执行（模型实现/前向计算）与共享层（请求处理、状态管理、资源协调）分离，并以显式接口界定边界，使兼容的模型变更被局部化，同时让模型与部署配置复用通用服务机制；论文还描述了配套的生产实践，用于吸收模型演进带来的变更面并收敛故障影响范围（失败隔离与版本/配置演进流程）。

Result: 系统已从探索性原型演进为阿里内部共享生产引擎，支持约 100 个模型、日处理数百 T tokens 量级，运行于异构生产环境；论文给出架构如何适应模型演进与限制故障影响的生产经验。

Conclusion: 在超大规模生产语境下，推理引擎的关键竞争力不仅是 kernel 与调度性能，更是"可演进性"——通过接口抽象与共享服务层分离变更面、以工程流程约束故障影响。该工作为构建模块化、易演进、高可靠的推理系统提供了可复用的架构范式与经验教训。

Abstract: Large language models and inference techniques evolve rapidly, making model enablement a recurring production task rather than a one-time integration effort. At Alibaba, a shared inference infrastructure must keep pace with new models and serving capabilities while remaining reliable across heterogeneous production environments. To reconcile rapid evolution with production reliability, we present RTP-LLM, an open-source production inference engine developed and deployed at Alibaba. Its architecture separates model-specific execution from shared request processing, state management, and resource coordination. Explicit interfaces localize compatible model changes while allowing models and deployment configurations to reuse common serving mechanisms. RTP-LLM has evolved from an exploratory system into a shared production engine that supports $\sim$100 models and processes hundreds of trillions of tokens per day. We describe how its architecture and production practices accommodate model evolution and contain the impact of failures, and share lessons for building evolvable and reliable inference engines at enterprise scale.

</details>


### [64] [High-Throughput Private Information Retrieval with on-CPU Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02376)
*Gyuyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: AltoPIR 是首个利用 CPU 片内矩阵加速器（如 Intel AMX）的 PIR 系统，通过让加速器直接从 CPU cache 取数、将批内查询的乘法共享为矩阵运算，实现不依赖独立加速器的高吞吐私有信息检索，吞吐较现有 CPU 方案最高提升 50.1×，且可服务超出 GPU 显存容量的数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIR 需扫描整个数据库才能隐藏检索目标，代价极高；批处理本可摊薄扫描成本，但现有 CPU 实现收益有限——每条查询各自做乘法，SIMD 向量指令一次只能处理少量乘加，数据带宽与算力都不足。GPU/离散加速器虽能加速计算，却需要额外硬件，且受显存容量限制，数据库规模可扩展性差。作者希望在不引入离散加速器的前提下，用 CPU 片上矩阵引擎获得批处理 PIR 的高吞吐。

Method: 核心方法有三点：(1) 将批内多条查询的 PIR 计算重构为矩阵-矩阵乘法（GEMM）形式，把"每条查询独立乘法"变成可被矩阵加速单元高效吞吐的稠密张量运算；(2) 设计适合 AMX 分块（tile）寄存器与二维 systolic 结构的数据库布局与分块调度，使每个数据库字节在一次批量扫描中只从主存读取一次，供整个 batch 复用；(3) 中间乘积始终停留在 cache/寄存器层级，从不回写主存，并配合 cache 驻留的数据库分片与流水式供给，避免 memory-bound 瓶颈，使 AMX 单元持续满载；同时保持纯 CPU 部署，不受显存容量约束。

Result: 与现有 CPU 基 PIR 方案相比，吞吐最高提升 50.1×；并能服务规模超过单张 GPU 显存容量的数据库，同时无需额外离散加速器硬件。

Conclusion: CPU 片内矩阵加速器是 PIR 这类"带宽受限但可批量化"负载的有效落点：通过把批处理转化为 GEMM、并做 cache-resident 的流水供给，可以在通用服务器上取得接近加速器的吞吐，同时摆脱显存容量与额外硬件的约束。这为隐私计算类服务在现有 CPU 集群上的低成本、可弹性扩展部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Private information retrieval (PIR) hides which record a client retrieves by scanning the entire database, and batching is an effective technique to improve throughput by sharing this scan across queries. However, existing CPU-based solutions gain little from batching because each query still requires its own multiplications, and vector instructions perform only a few at a time. GPUs and other discrete accelerators can accelerate these computations, but require extra hardware and can constrain database size due to their limited memory capacity. In this paper, we present AltoPIR, the first PIR system that leverages on-CPU matrix accelerators such as Intel AMX. AltoPIR keeps the accelerator fed from the CPU caches, reading each database byte from memory once for all queries in a batch and never writing intermediate products to memory. This enables high-performance PIR through efficient batching without requiring discrete accelerators. Our evaluation shows that AltoPIR achieves up to 50.1x higher throughput than existing CPU-based solutions and serves databases larger than a GPU's memory.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [65] [mmWave UE Positioning Under Blockage: Limits for ISAC-Assisted Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00453)
*Seyed Bagher Hashemi Natanzi,Bo Tang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文研究"能否用服务用户的无线单元（RU）从上行 SRS 参考信号中把用户定位得足够准，以支撑 mmWave 主动切换"。推导了含同步误差与阵列标定误差的定位误差界（分已知/未知数字孪生反射路径两种），并在射线追踪街道峡谷的留出场景上测试了两类估计器。结论：视距双 RU 下阵列标定（而非带宽）是主瓶颈；遮挡会急剧恶化纯视距界，但已知反射可维持低界；简单主径估计器在遮挡附近失效，使主动切换劣于 3GPP 基线；地图辅助多径跟踪器将中位误差从 12 cm 降至 3.7 cm，在 ≥15 dB 链路裕量下消除了该显著劣化，但仍未超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mmWave 链路易被行人遮挡，主动切换需要在遮挡发生前完成链路切换，而前提是网络掌握用户位置。3GPP 基线切换依赖测量上报、时延大，无法做到"预判"。作者因此追问：现有 RU 的上行 SRS 能否提供足够精度的定位——并且要把同步误差、阵列标定误差这类工程上真实存在的系统误差纳入考虑，而不仅是理想 CRLB。

Method: (1) 推导定位误差下界，显式包含同步偏差与阵列标定误差，并区分"未知反射路径"与"由数字孪生提供的已知反射路径"两种情况；(2) 在射线追踪的街道峡谷环境中，对留出（held-out）场景做评估，比较两种估计器：简单主径（dominant-path）估计器与地图辅助多径跟踪器；(3) 以 3GPP 切换基线为对照，考察主动切换在遮挡附近的性能与所需链路裕量。

Result: 三点结论：(1) 双 RU 视距条件下，误差界由阵列标定误差主导，带宽不是限制因素；(2) 遮挡使纯视距误差界急剧恶化，而利用已知反射可使地图辅助界保持在低位；(3) 主径估计器在遮挡附近误差快速增大，导致主动切换反而差于 3GPP 基线；地图辅助多径跟踪器把中位误差从 12 cm 降到 3.7 cm，在链路裕量 ≥15 dB 时消除了这一显著惩罚，但尚未整体优于基线。

Conclusion: 该工作表明 SRS 上行定位在 mmWave 主动切换场景中"可行但未达标"：精度瓶颈在阵列标定与环境先验（数字孪生反射路径），而非信号带宽；估计器必须在遮挡区域依赖多径/地图辅助，否则主动切换会带来负收益。工程含义上，它给出了两个明确的可操作方向——提升阵列标定精度、以及把数字孪生/反射几何纳入定位与切换决策——并提醒单靠主径测距式定位在 NLOS 边界会失效。

Abstract: Proactive handover can save a millimeter-wave (mmWave) link before a passing body blocks it, but only if the network knows where the user is. We ask whether the radio units that serve a user can localize it accurately enough from its uplink sounding reference signals. We derive position error bounds that include synchronization and array calibration errors, with and without reflected paths known from a digital twin, and test two estimators in a ray-traced street canyon on held-out scenarios. Three findings stand out. First, with line of sight to both radio units, array calibration, not bandwidth, limits the bound. Second, blockage degrades the line-of-sight-only bound sharply, whereas known reflections keep the map-aided bound low. Third, a simple dominant-path estimator fails where it matters most: its errors grow sharply around blockage, and proactive handover becomes worse than the 3GPP baseline. A map-aided multipath tracker cuts the median error from 12 to 3.7 cm and removes this significant penalty at link margins of 15 dB and above, but does not yet beat the baseline.

</details>


### [66] [From Confounding to Counterfactuals: A Cross-Layer Causal Model for RAN Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00635)
*Artin Elhamirad,Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Ali Tajer*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出跨PHY/MAC/网络层的层次结构因果模型(H-SCM)，将小区级负载混杂与UE级响应异质性分离，用每UE反事实goodput替代观测条件均值求解PRB分配问题，仿真中反事实调度相对最优损失<0.5%（神经网络基线2.0–2.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAN中基于遥测数据学习"控制动作—KPI"关联来做资源分配，存在两类偏差：(1) 共享无线环境同时影响调度决策与结果，形成cell-load混杂，使估计效应偏高，资源被错误分配给无法受益的UE；(2) UE对同一决策响应异质，即便纠正平均效应也无法识别"哪些UE"能获益。已有因果推断工作未建模从分配到结果的跨层机制。

Method: 构建覆盖PHY、MAC、网络层的层次结构因果模型H-SCM，显式分离混杂与异质性；在H-SCM下形式化反事实调度问题，以每UE反事实goodput而非观测条件平均作为PRB分配依据；求解管线为：约束式因果发现（constrained causal discovery）→ 结构方程拟合 → abduction-action-prediction（反事实推断三步法）。

Result: 朴素观测估计将满载PRB响应高估73.3%；校正cell-load混杂后相对误差降至9.8%；对held-out干预的预测R²=0.988；反事实调度相对真值最优的总小区goodput平均相对损失<0.5%，神经网络基线为2.0–2.9%。

Conclusion: 因果结构建模（而非纯关联学习）能有效去除RAN调度中的混杂偏差并刻画UE级异质响应，在仿真中显著缩小与最优调度的差距；代价是需要因果发现与结构方程拟合的额外建模/训练开销及可观测性假设。

Abstract: Data-driven control in radio access networks (RANs) selects resource allocations using associations between control actions and key performance indicators (KPIs) learned from network telemetry. Because the shared radio environment influences both the scheduler's decisions and the resulting performance, a bias known as confounding, such associations overstate the effect of an allocation and misdirect resources toward user equipment (UEs) that cannot benefit. Moreover, individual UEs respond heterogeneously to the same decision, so even a corrected average effect does not identify which UEs gain from additional resources. While causal inference has recently been applied to RANs, existing approaches do not model the cross-layer mechanisms that connect an allocation to its outcome. In this paper, a hierarchical structural causal model (H-SCM) spanning the PHY, MAC, and network layers is proposed that separates cell-load confounding from UE-specific response heterogeneity. In particular, a counterfactual scheduling problem is formulated in which physical resource block (PRB) allocations are selected using per-UE counterfactual goodput rather than observational conditional averages. This problem is solved via a pipeline combining constrained causal discovery, structural-equation fitting, and abduction-action-prediction. Simulations demonstrate that naive observational estimation overstates the full-PRB response by 73.3%, whereas adjustment for cell-load confounding reduces the relative error to 9.8%. Held-out interventions are predicted with a coefficient of determination of 0.988. Counterfactual scheduling achieves a mean relative loss in total cell goodput below 0.5% against the ground-truth optimum, compared with 2.0-2.9% for a neural-network baseline.

</details>


### [67] [Joint Trajectory, Sensing, and Computation Co-Design for UAV-Assisted Federated Edge Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00790)
*Kaichen Liu,Yueling Che,Sheng Luo,Jianhua Tang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 面向无人机辅助的联邦边缘视觉目标检测训练，提出"轨迹—感知—计算"四阶段联合协同设计，在电池约束下最小化期望训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将训练数据视为静态，忽略了动态视觉感知与机载重计算负载之间的紧耦合；而目标检测模型计算量大，无人机受电池与算力双重限制，需要同时考虑数据采集动态性与本地训练时延。

Method: 1）建立四阶段（感知/飞行/本地计算/通信聚合）联合的轨迹、感知与计算协同优化框架，以电池能量为约束最小化期望训练时间；2）用均匀感知概率刻画数据异构性，将联邦收敛性要求转化为无人机移动性约束；3）设计两层交替优化（AO）算法，自适应确定本地数据工作量，在所需训练轮数与机载计算时延之间取得平衡。

Result: 仿真表明，在模型收敛速度与最终精度上均优于基线方案。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将移动感知的数据分布特性显式嵌入联邦学习的收敛约束，并与轨迹、计算资源联合调度，可有效缓解数据异构下的收敛退化，同时降低电池受限平台的整体训练时延。

Abstract: This paper investigates UAV-assisted federated edge vision for training computationally heavy object detection models. Existing studies treat training data as static, ignoring the tight coupling between dynamic visual sensing and massive in place computational burdens. To minimize the expected training time under battery constraints, we propose a four-phase joint trajectory, sensing, and computation co-design. To mitigate data heterogeneity, we use a uniform sensing probability to translate federated convergence requirements into UAV mobility constraints. Furthermore, a two-layer alternating optimization (AO) algorithm is developed to adaptively determine the local data workload, optimally balancing the required training rounds against in-place computation delays. Simulations validate the superiority of our approach in model convergence and accuracy over baselines.

</details>


### [68] [Energy-Efficient Geographic Multicast Routing in Cognitive Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01212)
*Farzan Heidari,Farzaneh Heidari*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 面向认知无线传感器网络，提出两种地理组播路由协议 CCast 与 PCast：CCast 将组播树构建与面向时延的频谱选择度量结合，PCast 采用归一化的链路功率代价选路并可规避主用户占用低频段的区域；二者仅依赖局部邻居信息与目的坐标，无需全局拓扑。仿真中相比 GMP 节能 21%、26%，但单目的地路径更长、投递时延更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线传感器网络以能耗为首要设计约束；认知无线电提供对授权频谱的机会式接入，组播路由可让多目的地共享转发路径，二者结合有望同时降低能耗与频谱浪费。但已有地理组播路由（如 GMP）未考虑认知场景下的频谱异质性、主用户保护与链路功率差异。

Method: 两种基于地理信息（无状态、局部邻居 + 目的坐标）的组播协议：CCast 在组播树构建过程中嵌入时延导向的频谱选择度量；PCast 以归一化链路功率作为代价度量进行路由选择，并主动避开主用户占用低频段（长距离传输更省功率）的区域。仿真对比基线为 GMP 与多份单播路由，评估能耗、路径长度与投递时延。

Result: 相对 GMP，CCast 与 PCast 分别降低能耗 21% 与 26%；代价是每目的地路径变长、端到端投递时延升高。协议不依赖完整网络拓扑图，具备可扩展性与对拓扑变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：在认知无线传感器网络中，将频谱选择与功率归一化代价纳入地理组播决策，可在无全局拓扑的前提下换取显著能效收益，属于能耗—时延的显式折中。其系统价值主要在于把“频谱机会/主用户回避”纳入路由代价模型，对多播/组播通信的能效优化有参考意义；但对数据中心训练、推理serving、分布式系统、编译与内核、加速器、集群网络、存储与调度等 AI 基础设施实践的直接工程影响有限——它属于 WSN 与认知无线电层面，而非大模型系统栈；可迁移的经验是“局部信息驱动的分布式路由 + 归一化链路代价/资源可用性感知”这一设计范式。

Abstract: Energy consumption is a major design concern in wireless sensor networks. Cognitive radio provides opportunistic access to licensed spectrum, while multicast routing allows several destinations to share forwarding paths. In this paper, we propose two geographic multicast routing protocols for cognitive wireless sensor networks, CCast and PCast. CCast combines multicast-tree construction with a latency-oriented spectrum-selection metric. PCast uses a normalized link-power cost to select routes and can avoid regions where primary users occupy lower-frequency bands. Both protocols use local neighbor information and destination coordinates, without requiring a complete network-topology map. Simulations compare the proposed protocols with geographic multicast routing (GMP) and multiple unicast routing. In simulations, CCast and PCast reduce energy consumption by 21\% and 26\% relative to GMP, at the cost of longer per-destination routes and higher delivery delay.

</details>


### [69] [Vehicle and UAV Detection and Tracking with LTE Downlink Reference Signals: A Ray-Traced Evaluation on a Mapped Suburban Site with Licensed Base-Station Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01459)
*Arun K. Gurung,Satha K. Sathananthan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文研究蜂窝下行被动感知（passive sensing）：接收机置于用户设备侧、对网络侧零改动。基于LTE Band-3小区专用参考信号（CRS）与射线追踪信道给出仿真结果，并在合成街道峡谷与基于OSM与站点记录的实测场地两种场景下评估检测与覆盖性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蜂窝感知多依赖上行探测参考信号（SRS）或需网络侧改造（如专用感知波形、基站协作、信道状态上报），部署成本高、与现网兼容性差。作者希望验证一条"零侵入"路径：仅用UE侧被动接收下行CRS即可完成目标检测与轨迹跟随，从而在不改动运营商网络的前提下复用现网资源。

Method: 1) 信号臂：LTE Band-3 CRS（下行，UE侧被动接收），对照臂为同频段上行SRS；2) 信道：射线追踪（ray-traced）建模，含合成街道峡谷与基于OpenStreetMap建筑轮廓与授权站点记录的实测场地重建；3) 评价指标：漏检概率（missed-detection probability）、接收点覆盖率、可跟踪轨迹长度与目标高度分离；4) 场景：街道峡谷对照实验；实测场地沿街9个接收点跟踪车辆；283 m环线区分1.6–30 m目标高度与轨迹；137 m环线同时跟踪地面车辆与15 m空中目标；8个间距135 m的接收机由单个照射源覆盖1140 m街道。

Result: 合成街道峡谷中，下行CRS臂漏检概率0.667，上行SRS臂0.772（同频段、同目标、同评分器），下行被动臂略优；实测场地中9个接收点对车辆覆盖率为29.9%–61.7%；283 m环线可将目标高度与轨迹分离在1.6–30 m；137 m环线在所有小区上同时跟踪车辆与15 m空中目标；8个接收机以135 m间距实现单照射源覆盖1140 m街道。

Conclusion: 下行CRS被动感知在仿真层面可达到与上行SRS相当的检测性能，且无需任何网络侧改动，具备低成本、易部署的现网复用潜力；接收机布设密度与几何（间距135 m）是覆盖扩展的关键杠杆。后续需扩展到多照射源与不同接收机配置，并通过空口实测验证仿真结论。

Abstract: Passive sensing on the cellular downlink places the receiver at the user equipment and changes nothing in the network. We report simulation results for an LTE band-3 cell-specific reference signal arm on a ray-traced channel. On a synthetic street canyon the arm is compared with an uplink sounding reference signal arm on the same band, target and scorer: missed-detection probabilities of 0.667 and 0.772. On a surveyed site built from OpenStreetMap footprints and the licensed site record, nine receiver positions along the street track the vehicle at coverage between 29.9 % and 61.7 %; a 283 m lap separates target height from trajectory at 1.6 to 30 m; a 137 m lap on the built-up street tracks a vehicle and a 15 m airborne target on every cell, and 8 receivers at 135 m spacing carry one illuminator's coverage over 1140 m of the street. Potential future work includes extending the analysis to multiple illuminators and varying receiver configurations, and confirmation through over-the-air measurements.

</details>


### [70] [MATE: Multi-Agent Traffic Engineering for Topology-Aware Distributed SRv6 Scheduling under Bursty Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01831)
*Yuang Chen,Shuyi Liu,Chengdi Lu,Fengqian Guo,Hancheng Lu,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MATE 是一种面向部分 SRv6 部署混合网络的多智能体流量工程方法，采用"离线集中训练 + 在线分布执行"架构，利用流量稳定性刻画突发风险、路径敏感度量化承载能力，实现差异化主动调度；在线由边缘智能体本地生成分流策略并通过 SRv6 下发，规避集中式控制的控制环长延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络流量日益动态、突发，传统集中式 TE 依赖状态聚合、集中计算与策略分发，控制环延迟过长，难以应对突发流量；同时混合网络中 SRv6 只能部分部署，集中式全局优化在每个调度周期都不可行。

Method: 1) 用历史流量稳定性表征每条需求的突发风险，用路径敏感度量化候选路径吸收额外流量的能力，实现按流量特性差异化主动调度；2) 采用 CTDE 架构：训练阶段共享全局 critic 在"MLU + 变异性加权路径敏感度"的联合奖励下评估联合动作，并以反事实差分奖励（counterfactual difference reward）为各 actor 提供个体信用分配；3) 在线阶段边缘智能体仅凭本地观测生成分流策略并经 SRv6 执行，每个调度区间内不做集中优化。

Result: 在 CENI 试验床与 Sanet 拓扑仿真上，多数场景优于无个体信用分配的多智能体 RL 消融基线、COPE 和 TEAL，在实测流量轨迹上接近集中式 FIGRET；CENI-Abilene 上最多 90.1% 的测试实例 MLU 在 LP 最优值的 10% 以内；多数故障场景下归一化 MLU 中位数低于 FIGRET；平均神经推理时延 2.05–3.14 ms。

Conclusion: 论文表明：以反事实多智能体 RL 结合流量突发风险先验，可在部分 SRv6 部署的实际网络上以毫秒级在线推理获得接近集中式最优的 MLU，并具备故障鲁棒性，为分布式、低控制环延迟的主动 TE 提供了可行路径。

Abstract: As network traffic becomes increasingly dynamic and bursty, conventional centralized traffic engineering (TE) suffers from long control-loop delays caused by network-state aggregation, centralized computation, and policy distribution. In this paper, we propose MATE, a multi-agent TE method for proactive distributed scheduling in hybrid networks with partial deployment of Segment Routing over IPv6 (SRv6). MATE uses historical traffic stability to characterize the burst risk of each traffic demand and path sensitivity to quantify the ability of candidate paths to accommodate additional traffic, thereby enabling differentiated proactive scheduling tailored to heterogeneous traffic characteristics. MATE adopts an offline-centralized training and online-distributed execution architecture. During training, a shared global critic evaluates joint actions under a reward combining maximum link utilization (MLU) with variability-weighted path sensitivity, while counterfactual difference rewards provide agent-specific credit for actor learning. During online execution, edge agents generate traffic-splitting policies from local observations and apply them through SRv6, avoiding centralized optimization in each scheduling interval. Experiments on the China Environment for Network Innovations (CENI) testbed and simulations on the Sanet topology show that MATE outperforms a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) ablation baseline without agent-specific credit assignment, COPE, and TEAL in most evaluated scenarios and approaches centralized FIGRET on measured traffic traces. On CENI-Abilene, MATE achieves MLU within 10% of the linear-programming optimum in up to 90.1% of test instances. MATE also achieves lower median normalized MLU than FIGRET in most tested failure scenarios, with mean neural-inference times of 2.05--3.14~ms.

</details>


### [71] [Interoperability Assessment of Fronthaul and E2 Interfaces for Multi-Vendor Open RAN Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01862)
*Farhad Mehran,Charles Turyagyenda,Dritan Kaleshi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 基于O-RAN联盟IOT规范，在SONIC实验室对13套多厂商Open RAN系统进行Open Fronthaul与E2接口的互操作测试评估，总结最新发现与工程经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Open RAN通过开放接口、解耦与虚拟化RAN组件来改造无线网络架构，但多厂商组件的接口集成极为困难：Open Fronthaul需要对齐IOT profile参数，而E2接口因Near-RT RIC是RAN架构中新引入的组件，存在大量未预见的集成问题。因此需要严格的互操作评估来加速可互操作方案成熟。

Method: 依据O-RAN Alliance IOT规范，在SmartRAN Open Network Interoperability Centre (SONIC) Labs中对13套来自不同厂商的Open RAN系统开展Open Fronthaul与E2接口的互操作评估，并归纳测试中的发现与经验教训。

Result: 摘要未给出量化结果，主要产出为多厂商环境下Open Fronthaul与E2接口互操作性的实证性发现与经验总结（属测量/经验类研究）。

Conclusion: 多厂商Open RAN的接口互操作仍是落地瓶颈，需严格对齐Fronthaul IOT profile并针对E2/Near-RT RIC集成中的未预见问题建立系统化的测试与规范实践。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Networks (RANs) aim to transform the existing wireless network architectures by adopting open interfaces, disaggregating and virtualising RAN components, and enabling dynamic control of the system via data-driven methods. To accelerate the development of interoperable RAN solutions, rigorous assessment of Open Fronthaul and E2 interfaces based on O-RAN Alliance Interoperability Testing (IOT) specifications plays a key role as the integration of these interfaces seems to be very challenging, e.g. due to the need for alignment of Fronthaul IOT profiles and unforeseen challenges for E2 interface integration as Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT RIC) is relatively a new component introduced within the RAN architecture. In this paper, selected parts of the latest findings and lessons learned from interoperability assessment of Open Fronthaul and E2 interfaces for 13 multi-vendor Open RAN systems deployed in SmartRAN Open Network Interoperability Centre (SONIC) Labs are presented.

</details>


### [72] [PRISM: RL-Based Rate Control for Low Latency and High Data Rate in Mobile Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02032)
*Hamid Hassani,Staji Wilson,Kishor C. Joshi,Sonia M. Heemstra,Ignas G. Niemegeers,George Exarchakos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: PRISM 是一个基于强化学习的拥塞控制算法（CCA），它放弃传统的丢包/时延/ECN 信号，改用移动网络中的物理资源块（PRB）峰值利用率作为早期拥塞信号来调节发送速率，从而在高移动性与衰落、强竞争环境下同时维持高吞吐与低时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR、远程操作等时延敏感交互式业务要求移动网络中的 CCA 既能保持高速率又要压低时延。现有 CCA 依赖的拥塞信号存在缺陷：丢包与排队时延信号滞后且不精确，ECN 需要网络侧大范围升级部署。因此需要一种来自无线接入网内部、更早、更可靠的拥塞信号。

Method: 以 PRB 利用率（峰值 PRB utilization）作为反映无线侧真实拥塞程度的早期信号，构建基于 RL 的 CCA，将发送速率调节建模为决策问题，在移动性与信道衰落条件下学习速率控制策略；在 NS3 中与 TCP Cubic、TCP BBR 共存的移动场景下评估。

Result: 在两条 PRISM 流与两条 TCP Cubic、一条 TCP BBR 共存的移动场景中，20 分钟高竞争区间内 PRISM 的单包平均单向时延 6.3ms、p99 为 44.6ms、丢包率约 0.02%；同期共存的 TCP 流平均 OWD 达 371–390ms（约 60 倍）、p99 为 563–578ms、丢包 0.1%；goodput-over-latency 指标提升 31 倍。

Conclusion: PRB 利用率是移动网络中有用的早期拥塞信号，基于它的 RL CCA 能在强竞争与移动条件下为时延敏感业务取得更优的吞吐-时延折中。局限在于结果仅来自 NS3 仿真、缺少真实 RAN/终端部署与跨运营商验证，且需要 UE 能获取 PRB 利用率的网络侧接口或跨层测量，实际落地需考虑信号粒度、时延与控制回路带宽。

Abstract: Emerging delay-sensitive interactive services, such as extended reality and remote operation, demand advanced congestion control algorithms (CCAs) capable of maintaining high data rates while keeping latency low in mobile networks. Existing CCAs typically rely on congestion signals such as packet loss, delay, or explicit congestion notification (ECN) marks, which can be delayed, imprecise, or require extensive upgrades to the network infrastructure. To address these limitations, physical resource block (PRB) utilization has recently been proposed as a reliable early congestion signal in mobile networks [1]. This paper introduces PRISM, a reinforcement-learning-based CCA that uses peak PRB utilization to regulate the send rate under challenging mobility and fading conditions. NS3 simulation results show that, in a mobility scenario where two PRISM flows coexist with two TCP Cubic flows and one TCP BBR flow, PRISM maintains a per-packet mean one-way delay (OWD) of 6.3ms and a $p_{99}$ OWD of 44.6ms, with negligible (0.02%) packet loss, over a 20min high-contention interval. However, over the same interval, the coexisting TCP flows experience 60x higher mean OWDs of 371-390ms and $p_{99}$ OWDs of 563-578ms, with 0.1% packet loss. PRISM achieves a 31x improvement in goodput-over-latency performance, indicating a favorable tradeoff for delay-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [73] [QueueTrack: Framework for Detecting the Onset of Queueing in Mobile Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02038)
*Hamid Hassani,Anudeep Karnam,Kishor Joshi,Sonia M. Heemstra,Ignas G. Niemegeers,George Exarchakos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: QueueTrack 提出一种端主机可观测的排队起始检测框架：从端到端可观测的包级时序统计中构造排队指示量 QBI，用轻量 ML 分类器（如逻辑回归）在 5G OAI 测试床上判定基站侧瓶颈队列是否开始积压，混合 TCP/UDP 场景下 F1≈80%，无需端主机间紧同步、无需改网络设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络中端主机对路径瓶颈队列状态可见性极低，传统拥塞信号（丢包、RTT、OWD）在"排队起始/早期积压"阶段的检测能力有已知缺陷：丢包滞后且破坏性、RTT/OWD 受排队以外的波动（调度、切换、无线链路变化、缓冲膨胀）污染且需低噪声同步。作者希望在不改造基础设施的前提下，为低时延 CCA、遥测与监控提供更早、更可靠的排队信号。

Method: 核心是构造 QBI（排队积压指示量）：基于端点自身可测的包级时序统计（如单向/往返时延序列的离散度与变化率、包对/包串间隔、连续包时延差分等统计特征），不要求端主机与基站紧同步，也不依赖网内探针。论文给出完整 ML 流水线：数据采集、标注（以可观测的队列状态/基站侧信息为标签）、特征工程与模型训练、测试评估；在 OpenAirInterface 的 5G 测试床上覆盖多种存在竞争 TCP 流的场景（含混合 TCP/UDP），对比不同复杂度分类模型。

Result: 在 5G OAI 测试床上，低复杂度模型即可稳定检出基站排队起始；最具挑战的混合 TCP/UDP 竞争场景下 F1≈80%（约 80%）。说明该信号不依赖重模型，端主机侧开销可接受。

Conclusion: QueueTrack 表明仅凭端主机时序统计即可在移动网络中较早、可靠地检测排队起始，可作为低时延 CCA 的拥塞信号来源，也适用于网络遥测与监控。其局限性在于依赖特定测试床（单基站、OAI、有限场景）、标签可得性及跨场景（不同 RAT、移动性、跨运营商、多瓶颈）泛化尚未验证；作为训练/服务基础设施的直接价值更多在于为调度与流量整形、故障定位提供低开销的端侧可观测信号。

Abstract: In mobile networks, timely and accurate detection of the onset of queueing at path bottlenecks remains challenging for end-to-end congestion control algorithms (CCAs), where end hosts have limited visibility into queue status. Traditional congestion signals, such as packet loss, round-trip time (RTT), and one-way delay (OWD), have well-known limitations for early queue buildup detection. This paper presents QueueTrack, a practical end-host framework to detect the onset of queueing in mobile networks. QueueTrack computes a queue buildup indicator, denoted QBI, from endpoint-observable packet-level timing statistics, without requiring tight synchronization between end hosts or modifications to the existing network infrastructure. We develop and assess the complete QueueTrack machine learning (ML) pipeline, including data collection, labeling, model training, and testing, on a 5G testbed built on the OpenAirInterface platform, under a range of scenarios with competing TCP traffic. The experimental results show that QueueTrack can reliably detect the onset of queueing at the 5G base station using low-complexity classification models such as logistic regression. For example, QueueTrack achieves an F1 score of approximately 80% in a challenging mixed TCP/UDP scenario. These findings confirm that QueueTrack can serve as a practical framework for developing CCAs that target low delay, as well as for network telemetry and network monitoring in mobile networks.

</details>


### [74] [Band-Separated Redundant Communication for a Mars-Analog Rover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02131)
*Zamiul Rashid,Akid Anis,Tanzim Ahmed,Md. Golam Rabiul Alam,Md. Khalilur Rhaman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文描述并外场实测了 BRACU Mongol-Tori 火星车（URC 竞赛）的通信栈：将视频/RTK 回传与控制指令分离到不同电台与频段，回传链路为 5.2 GHz 主链路 + 2.4 GHz 备链路并由 Babel 路由协议选路，控制指令同时在两个 sub-GHz 电台双发、备路仅在主路静默时启用；通过断电与注入丢包的台架实验、以及带非视距段的 >1 km 外场行驶，验证了故障切换时延与总吞吐覆盖，并暴露了 Babel 仅以 Hello 丢包判链的度量缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: URC 参赛队远程超视距操控火星车，赛事方指出通信失效是最常见的失败原因。火星车需要同时承载多路相机视频的带宽，以及一条在单电台失效后仍能存活的指令通路。论文动机是用低成本、易复现的 COTS 射频组合与现成路由协议满足这一"带宽 + 控制韧性"的双重需求，并量化其在真实信道条件下的表现。

Method: 1) 架构：功能面分离——视频与 RTK 差分修正走 5.2 GHz 网桥（2.4 GHz 备份），由 Babel 在两者间选路；控制指令走双 sub-GHz 电台同步冗余发送，接收端以"超时静默"作为主路失效判据进行选择。2) 台架实验：人为断电主链路、注入丢包（含 10% 丢包与主路接收机故障），观察切换时间、是否误切与回切（flapping）。3) 外场实验：驶离基站 >1 km，约半程起进入非视距，记录主备链路各自失效距离与备路可用性。4) 通过 Babel 的 metric offset 偏置调参抑制不必要的切换。

Result: 台架上注入断电/丢包后，流量在数秒内切至备路；通过 metric offset 可使链路在主路 10% 丢包时仍停留在主路（避免震荡）。外场：主链路在 771 m 处失效，备链路将视频与 RTK 修正维持到略超 1 km（此时已处非视距）。同时暴露 Babel 局限：其链路评判仅依赖 Hello 消息丢失，未反映实际容量，导致主路接收机故障（备路实测容量约为其 2 倍）时仍保持主路；并在作用距离边缘出现链路抖动/反复切换。

Conclusion: 结论是"控制面与数据面分频段、控制指令双发 + 静默检测"的工程方案在低成本火星车场景中有效且可复现，但通用 MANET 路由协议（Babel）以 Hello 丢包为唯一度量，无法感知容量与硬件劣化，会在边缘与故障态下做出错误选路。作者给出天线对准、链路度量设计（应纳入吞吐/RSSI/误码等信号）与完整链路日志三项经验，并建议以应用层/自定义 metric 补充或替换默认协议判据。

Abstract: Teams in the University Rover Challenge (URC) drive their rovers remotely and often out of sight. The organizers name communication failure as the most common reason a team falls short. A rover therefore needs bandwidth for several camera views and a command path that survives the loss of one radio. We describe and field test the communication stack of the BRACU Mongol-Tori rover, which keeps vision and control on separate radios and bands. Video and RTK corrections travel over a 5.2 GHz bridge with a 2.4 GHz backup, and the Babel routing protocol chooses between them. Every command goes out on two sub-gigahertz radios at once, and the rover uses the backup copy only when the primary falls silent. We tested the backhaul on the bench with a power cut and injected packet loss. We also drove the rover more than a kilometer from the base, and line of sight ended about halfway. On the bench, traffic reached the backup within a few seconds. A metric offset kept it on a primary that lost one packet in ten. In the field, the primary failed at 771 m and the backup carried video and corrections to just over a kilometer. The drive also exposed a limit of Babel, which judges a link only by lost Hello messages. It kept traffic on a primary with a faulty receiver, although the backup reported twice the capacity, and it flapped at the edge of range. We close with lessons on antenna aiming, link metrics and logging.

</details>


### [75] [Wireless Networks That See: Integrated Sensing and Communication in Complex Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02157)
*Xingqin Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该文是一篇关于ISAC（通感一体化）的教程性文章，系统讲解如何利用已知蜂窝信号把“无线信道中的干扰性多径”转化为可用的目标观测能力，并给出一套工程化的观测链路：传播参数估计→静止杂波抑制→反射/虚拟目标甄别→多点协作融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：蜂窝网络天然存在大量无线电交互，这些交互既干扰通信、也携带环境信息；ISAC希望把这些交互变成对UAV、道路车辆、工业移动机器人等目标的感知能力。但作者指出的核心问题是“强回波≠可靠观测”：1) 静止背景（强直达波/静态杂波）会淹没目标运动；2) 反射路径会产生虚拟/鬼影目标；3) 单站点单视角导致目标运动分量不可观测（几何可观测性不足）。因此需要方法论层面的梳理，而非单一算法性能提升。

Method: 方法：教程式流程化方法。1) 利用已知的蜂窝参考信号（同步/导频/参考符号）做信道传播参数估计（时延、多普勒、角度等），把感知问题统一到信道参数域；2) 静止背景抑制——通过多普勒/时延域滤波、静态分量对消去除零多普勒的静态杂波；3) 反射路径判读——利用几何一致性与多径结构区分真实目标与由镜面反射造成的虚拟目标；4) 多站点协作——融合不同基站的观测，补偿单视角下不可观测的运动分量；5) 局部一致性校验（local checks）作为后处理门限以压制虚假输出。验证基于3GPP随机信道模型下的UAV场景，以及旧金山城市场景的射线追踪（ray tracing），用空间布局、信道剖面、距离-多普勒图前后对比来说明各机制的作用。

Result: 结果：1) 局部校验能显著减少虚假输出，但代价是检测率的部分损失（明确的precision/recall权衡）；2) 对同时被单站与协作站点检测到的UAV，多站协作提升了定位精度；3) 案例展示了信号资源（参考信号配置、带宽/时长）、测量上报方式与应用需求三者共同决定感知性能，说明感知指标是资源配置的函数而非算法固有属性。注意：文章是教程性质的机制阐释，未给出大规模统一量化基准。

Conclusion: 结论：复杂环境下的ISAC感知应从“追求更强回波”转向“保证观测可靠性”，即通过传播建模、静态杂波抑制、多径语义判读、多点融合与局部一致性校验构建可解释的处理链；同时必须把信号资源与测量上报机制纳入感知服务的设计空间。对工程实践的启示：感知性能评估需与3GPP信道模型和真实城市场景射线追踪结合，多站协作是提升可观测性与定位精度的关键杠杆，而误检抑制与检测率的权衡应作为可调的服务质量旋钮。

Abstract: The same radio interactions that complicate wireless communication can reveal what is happening around a network. Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) turns these interactions into opportunities to observe unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), road vehicles, and industrial mobile robots. Yet a strong echo is not necessarily a reliable object observation: stationary background can conceal motion, reflections can create virtual objects, and one viewpoint can leave part of an object's motion unobserved. This tutorial explains how known cellular signals are used to estimate propagation, suppress stationary background, interpret reflected paths, and combine observations from different sites. UAV examples under a Third Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) stochastic channel model and ray tracing in a San Francisco urban scene illustrate the mechanisms through spatial layouts, channel profiles, and before/after range--Doppler maps. Local checks reduce false outputs, with some loss of detection. Cooperation improves position accuracy for UAVs detected by both a single site and cooperating sites. The examples show how signal resources, measurement reporting, and application needs shape sensing performance, providing practical guidance for sensing services in complex environments.

</details>


### [76] [S-R3: Secure and Robust RIS-Assisted RSMA under Finite-Resolution Phase Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02662)
*Ibrahima Sory Diallo,Elhadj Moustapha Diallo,Wang Mi,Mamadou Aliou Diallo,Amayika Kakati*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文研究不完美窃听者CSI与有限分辨率RIS相位下的RIS辅助RSMA下行安全传输，提出联合设计预编码、各向同性人工噪声、公共速率分配与RIS相位的鲁棒优化框架，并用保守泄漏界、凸内层更新、可行性初始化与有限候选搜索求解，给出了收敛性与外层更新有限性的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIS可重塑信道、RSMA可灵活分配公共/私有流功率，两者结合有望在存在窃听者时兼顾保密与公共吞吐；但实际系统中窃听者信道CSI有界误差、RIS相位量化有限，直接按名义CSI设计会破坏安全性与可行性，需要跨RIS相位更新的有效信道不确定性刻画与鲁棒设计。

Method: 系统模型：RIS辅助RSMA下行，公共流承载公开信息、私有流承载机密信息；采用保守安全评估，允许每个窃听者消除公共流并把其他用户私有流作为边信息。优化变量为发射预编码、各向同性人工噪声、公共速率分配与RIS相位，目标为保密吞吐与公共吞吐的折中，受速率与功率约束。针对直达与级联窃听信道的有界误差，导出对所有RIS相位更新均有效的有效信道不确定性界；构造保守泄漏上界与凸的内层发射机更新，通过可行性过程初始化，并在有限人工噪声功率与RIS相位候选中带显式可行性检查搜索。

Result: 理论结果：固定人工噪声功率与RIS配置时，可行内层迭代目标值单调不降且收敛、相邻迭代差趋于零；被接受的外层更新保持可行且数量有限（因候选集有限），从而保证算法返回可行解而非发散或产生不可行输出。

Conclusion: 论文给出了有限分辨率RIS相位与窃听CSI误差下的鲁棒安全RSMA设计流程，工程贡献在于将难处理的安全约束转化为可计算的保守泄漏界与凸子问题，并配套可行性初始化与候选搜索以保证可部署性；求解代价主要来自候选组合与鲁棒SDP/凸子问题求解，实际部署需权衡RIS相位量化位数、候选数量与优化时延。

Abstract: This paper investigates a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) downlink with imperfect eavesdropper channel state information and finite-resolution RIS phases. The common stream carries public information, while user-specific private streams carry confidential information. For a conservative security assessment, each eavesdropper is allowed to remove the public stream and is given the other private streams as side information. We formulate a robust design of t he transmit precoders, isotropicartificial noise, common-rate allocations, and RIS phases that balances confidential and public throughput under rate and power constraints. Bounded errors in the direct and cascaded eavesdropper channels yield an effective-channel uncertainty bound valid across RIS phase updates. To address the resulting nonconvex problem, we construct conservative leakage bounds and convex inner transmit updates, initialize through a feasibility procedure, and search finite artificial-noise and RIS-phase candidates with explicit feasibility checks. For a fixed artificialnoise power and RIS configuration, the feasible inner iterates have a nondecreasing objective that converges in value, and their successive changes vanish. Accepted outer updates remain feasible and are finite in number. Our code is available at https://github.com/ABM-ADAM/RIS RSMA-secure.

</details>


### [77] [Multipartite FSO/QKD with N-GHZ States for Scalable Secure Group Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02913)
*Yuma Kaminaga,Hoang D. Le,Cuong T. Nguyen,Anh T. Pham*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种高空平台（HAP）辅助的多方量子密钥分发（multipartite QKD）网络架构，将 N-GHZ 态协议与 FSO 信道上的 TDMA 方案结合，并用 Qiskit 仿真验证其在 6G 安全群组通信中相比传统两方方案可显著提升组密钥率并降低网络执行时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G 需要抗量子攻击的群组密钥生成机制；但传统 QKD 协议面向点对点链路，无法直接用于多方群密钥建立，缺少可扩展的多方 QKD 网络架构。

Method: 架构层：以 HAP 作为可信/中继节点承载多方纠缠分发，采用 N-GHZ 态协议实现多方（multipartite）密钥协商；媒质层：自由空间光（FSO）信道；接入层：TDMA 时分复用支持多用户共享星地/空链路，隔离多方测量与分发时序。评估层：基于 Qiskit 的仿真框架，注入 FSO 物理层损伤（衰减、湍流、对准误差等），统计组密钥率（SKR）与网络执行时延，并做波束腰（beam-waist）等参数的优化分析。

Result: 仿真显示该架构相对传统两方（bipartite）方案显著提升群组 SKR，并有效降低整体网络执行时延；同时量化了信道损伤对性能的影响，给出最优波束腰控制等工程设计准则。

Conclusion: HAP 辅助多方 QKD 可作为面向大规模 6G 安全群组通信的可行方案；但当前结论主要基于 Qiskit 仿真框架而非真实链路或硬件实验，缺少与经典多方密钥协商/后量子 PQC 方案的端到端开销与成本对比，其系统级价值仍需在链路预算、时隙调度开销与量子存储/探测器件约束下进一步验证。

Abstract: The emergence of sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks is driving the need for secure group key generation mechanisms resilient to quantum-computing attacks. Conventional quantum key distribution (QKD) protocols are designed for point-to-point communication and are therefore not directly applicable to group key establishment. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a scalable High-Altitude Platform (HAP)-assisted multipartite QKD network architecture that integrates $N$-GHZ-state protocols with a Time Division Multiple Access (TDMA) scheme over free-space optical (FSO) channels. The performance of the proposed system is evaluated using a Qiskit-based simulation framework that incorporates realistic physical-layer impairments of FSO links. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed architecture significantly improves the group secret key rate (SKR) compared to conventional bipartite approaches, while effectively reducing overall network execution delay. In addition, the analysis reveals the impact of channel impairments and provides practical design insights, such as optimal beam-waist control. The proposed framework demonstrates the potential of HAP-assisted multipartite QKD as an effective security solution for future large-scale 6G wireless networks supporting secure group communications.

</details>


### [78] [DisC: Incentive Design for Green, Robust, and Fair Client Self-Participation in OTA Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03024)
*Driss Choukri,Essaid Sabir,Abdelkrim Haqiq*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出 DisC：面向 OTA-FL 的博弈论分布式客户端选择框架，用势博弈的纳什均衡实现公平、信道感知与能耗高效的参与决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTA-FL 利用无线多址信道的波形叠加提升通信效率，但客户端异质性、衰落、能耗约束与参与不均衡会同时损害公平性与聚合精度。现有 Pareto 公平 OTA-FL 在"高权重客户端信道差"时仍会出现大的聚合失真（信道噪声被高权重放大），且集中式选择缺乏可扩展性与激励相容性。

Method: 1) 每个客户端以分布式方式选择"参与水平"（participation level，可理解为参与频率/发送功率/子载波或资源投入的离散档位），最大化一个效用函数；2) 效用融合五项：Pareto 公平重要性权重、学习相关性（梯度/更新质量）、OTA 叠加失真、能耗、时延；3) 理论证明该博弈存在纳什均衡，并可构造成精确势博弈（exact potential game），其稳定均衡对应 OTA-FL 的局部最优运行点；4) 借此把集中式客户端选择替换为分布式、激励相容的自组织决策。

Result: 理论结果：DisC 的博弈存在纳什均衡且为精确势博弈，均衡点与 OTA-FL 局部最优工作点对应，从而保证分布式决策收敛到有意义的系统运行点。摘要未给出收敛速度、聚合失真下降幅度、公平性指标（如 Jain 指数）或能耗对比的量化数据。

Conclusion: DisC 的核心贡献是把 OTA-FL 的客户端选择问题形式化为精确势博弈，使公平性、信道状态与能耗约束在单次分布式决策中被统一优化，并具备激励相容性——相对于需要全局 CSI 与调度的集中式方案，更适合无线边缘场景的实际部署。

Abstract: Over-the-Air Federated Learning (OTA-FL) improves communication efficiency by exploiting waveform superposition over wireless Multiple-Access Channels (MAC). However, client heterogeneity, fading, energy constraints, and participation imbalance can degrade both fairness and aggregation accuracy. Existing Pareto-oriented fair OTA-FL schemes may still suffer from large aggregation distortion when highly weighted clients experience poor channel conditions. This paper proposes DisC, a game-theoretic distributed client selection framework for fairness-aware, channel-aware, and energy-efficient OTA-FL. Each client selects its participation level by maximizing a utility that captures Pareto fairness importance, learning relevance, OTA distortion, energy consumption, and delay. We show that DisC admits a Nash equilibrium and can be formulated as an exact potential game, where stable equilibria correspond to locally optimal OTA-FL operating points. DisC therefore offers a distributed, incentive-compatible alternative to centralized client selection under wireless and resource constraints.

</details>


### [79] [Pod-wise Multimodal Federated Learning for Heterogeneous IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03273)
*Joy Okolo,Zihao Ding,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: TIDE 提出"层级感知的 pod 内解耦编码器"联邦学习框架：每个传感器拥有独立编码器与辅助头，参数仅在持有该传感器的设备子集（pod）内聚合；融合层与任务头全局共享。解决 IoT 多模态联邦学习中参与者传感器集随时间轮次变化（部分设备缺传感器）导致的参数覆盖与单传感器设备无法学习跨模态关系的问题。在三个多模态活动识别数据集（ActionSense 等）上超过 8 个基线，ActionSense 达 78.06%（+7.63 点），且在自身通信量一半时仍为三数据集最高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT 联邦学习场景中设备异构传感器配置、且上线模式呈周期性，导致每一轮的参与者集合及其可用模态不断变化。朴素 FedAvg 式平均存在两个致命缺陷：(1) 不持有某传感器的设备会用自己的（不存在/无意义）参数覆盖该传感器参数，造成模态编码器被"污染"；(2) 只带单一传感器的设备只更新该模态编码器，永远学不到模态间关联，而融合层需要跨模态语义。已有两条路线各有硬假设：要么重新设计本地训练以支持不匹配传感器（但假设设备始终在线，与周期性上线矛盾），要么做客户端选择（但忽略设备携带哪些传感器，选到无该模态的设备仍无效）。核心诉求是：在没有聚合模式、设备轮次随机可用、模态覆盖不完整的条件下，仍能对每个模态编码器做正确聚合，并让每个设备（含单模态设备）都能获得有意义的跨模态融合能力。

Method: TIDE（Tier-aware Intra-pod Decoupled Encoders）采用"层级感知 + pod 内解耦"的两级参数架构：(1) 解耦编码器——每个传感器 s 拥有专属编码器 E_s 和辅助头 h_s，其参数只在 pod(s)（即本轮持有该传感器的设备集合）内部进行聚合，从而避免无该传感器的设备覆盖其参数，并使编码器只在语义一致的数据分布上做 FedAvg 式更新；(2) 分层聚合 tier-aware——按传感器可用性子集构造聚合层级/pod，并处理 pod 间重叠与设备同时属于多个 pod 的情形，使单轮内一个设备可对多个 pod 贡献更新；(3) 共享融合层与任务头——融合层 F 与任务头 g 由所有设备共享并在全体参与者上聚合，保证跨模态对齐知识与主任务语义在所有设备间传播，包括单传感器设备也能通过共享融合层获得跨模态关系；(4) 辅助头 h_s 在本地提供该模态的监督信号，稳定解耦编码器的表示学习，缓解仅在共享头反传时模态间梯度冲突与表示坍塌。整体实现为 per-round 的 pod 级模型下发、本地多任务训练（辅助损失 + 主任务损失）、pod 级与全局级的两步参数聚合。

Result: 在三个多模态活动识别数据集上与 8 个基线对比：ActionSense 上达 78.06%，比次优方法高 7.63 个百分点；在全部三个数据集上，以自身一半的通信开销即取得最高精度（即通信效率显著优于基线）；在同等通信预算下，于 ActionSense 领先，在另两个数据集上在多种子方差范围内领先（统计上持平或更优）。表明精度收益并非来自更大通信量。

Conclusion: 论文将 IoT 联邦学习的模态异构问题形式化为"随轮次变化的参与者传感器集合"下的聚合正确性问题，指出滤波式客户端选择与本地重设计两条既有路线均无法同时满足在线波动与跨模态学习需求。TIDE 通过 per-sensor pod 内聚合并共享融合/任务头的层级解耦架构，在不假设设备恒定在线的前提下同时解决参数覆盖与单模态设备学习跨模态关系两个问题，并以更少通信取得更高精度。系统层面启示：联邦训练的参数划分应显式对齐"数据/传感器可用性"这一物理层级，聚合拓扑需随参与者模态覆盖动态生成；该思路可直接迁移到边缘推理服务中的多模态模型增量更新、以及异构硬件/异构模态集群的分组同步与通信压缩调度。局限：pod 划分与融合层的梯度冲突处理、辅助头带来的本地计算与内存开销、以及极稀疏模态覆盖下的收敛性仍需进一步分析。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is well suited to Internet of Things (IoT) sensing. However, devices carry different sensors and come online in periodic patterns, and as such, the sensors among the participants change from round to round. Under naive averaging, devices without a sensor overwrite its parameters, and a single-sensor device never learns how its sensor relates to the others. Existing methods either redesign local training for mismatched sensors (assuming always-available devices) or select clients without regard to their sensors. We propose Tier-aware Intra-pod Decoupled Encoders (TIDE). Specifically, each sensor has its own encoder and auxiliary head, whose parameters are merged only within the "pod" (i.e., the devices that hold the sensor), while the fusion layer and the task head remain shared by all devices. On three multimodal activity recognition datasets against eight baselines, TIDE reaches 78.06% on ActionSense, 7.63 points above the next method, is the most accurate on all three datasets at half its own communication, and at equal communication leads on ActionSense and is ahead within seed variation on the other two.

</details>


### [80] [From Reactive to Agentic Resilience: Multi-Timescale AI-RAN Orchestration Under Signaling Storms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03406)
*Sotiris Chatzimiltis,Mahdi Boloursaz Mashhadi,Mohammad Shojafar,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文提出三层agentic AI框架来编排Open RAN/AI-RAN的弹性生命周期（预判-吸收-适应-恢复-演化），把慢速LLM推理移出快速控制环路：SMOrchestrator设定常驻策略、每站点非实时agent判定风暴并提前预判计划性人群、近实时确定性环路执行决策。基于自建的离散事件Attach仿真（含重试放大、共享算力争用、非瞬时供给），表明提前供给可用1/4容量逼近全量供给的弹性，而纯反应式调权在浪涌到来后无法补救；自由文本意图到控制指令的接地准确率94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Open RAN/AI-RAN将RAN变为解耦的compute-aware平台，服务连续性依赖多时间尺度控制环。现有弹性机制按阶段割裂（预判/吸收/适应/恢复/演化各自为政），缺乏跨控制层级的协调；同时若把LLM推理解析直接放到快速控制路径上，会引入不可接受的时延与不确定性。因此需要一种既能利用语义意图理解、又不损害近实时确定性的编排架构。

Method: (1) 以mass-attachment信令风暴为研究场景，构建离散事件Open RAN Attach仿真器，显式建模重试放大（retry amplification）、共享算力争用与非瞬时provisioning；(2) 提出三层框架：SMO层orchestrator产出standing policy并经自由文本意图接地为控制指令；每站点非实时RIC agent做风暴判定与日历驱动的crowd预判；近实时RIC确定性环路负责逐决策执行与强制约束；(3) 给出anticipation horizon定理及反应式供给极限的推论（解析证明置于补充材料）；(4) 实验量化时延、成本与争用边界。

Result: 通过读取日历事件并提前provisioning，agentic控制器以约1/4容量达到全量供给服务器池的弹性水平；浪涌到来后的任何反应式权重调整均无法恢复该性能。自由文本运营意图到控制指令的接地在未见意图上达94% exact match，单条指令可端到端改变在线行为。论文同时量化了限制该方案的时延、成本与争用上限。

Conclusion: 弹性应被建模为跨时间尺度、跨控制层级的生命周期编排问题，而非单阶段机制。合理分工是“慢思考在上、快执行在下”：LLM负责语义意图与预判，确定性近实时环路负责执行保证。提前（anticipatory）供给的收益远大于反应式控制，且存在可解析刻画的预判时域上界。

Abstract: Next-generation open radio access network (Open RAN) and AI-RAN deployments turn the RAN into a disaggregated, compute-aware platform whose service continuity depends on many control loops acting at different timescales. Resilience in this setting is best seen as a lifecycle spanning anticipation, absorption, adaptation, recovery, and evolution. Yet, existing mechanisms remain stage-specific and are rarely coordinated across the control hierarchy. This paper argues that agentic AI offers an orchestration layer that coordinates this lifecycle, turning operator intent into coordinated action while keeping slow language-model reasoning off the fast control path. Using mass-attachment signaling storms as a running study, we build a discrete-event Open RAN attach simulator that captures retry amplification, shared-compute contention, and non-instantaneous provisioning, and we propose a three-tier framework in which an orchestrator at the service management and orchestration layer sets a standing policy, a per-site non-real-time agent judges storms and anticipates scheduled crowds, and a deterministic near-real-time loop enforces every decision. Across experiments, the agentic controller approaches the resilience of a fully provisioned pool of servers at a quarter of its capacity by reading a scheduled event from a calendar and provisioning ahead of it. No reactive weight setting recovers this once the surge arrives. Free-text operator intents ground into control directives at 94% exact match on previously unseen intents, and a single instruction reshapes live behavior end to end. We also quantify the latency, cost, and contention limits that bound it. The supplementary material provides analytical proof of the derived Theorem on anticipation horizon, as well as the subsequent Corollary results on reactive provisioning limits.

</details>
